# Assinafy n8n Node — Operation Reference

Full request/response reference for every operation exposed by the **Assinafy** action node, including signer-side and authentication flows. Account-scoped paths use the **Account ID** from the credential (shown as `{accountId}`). List operations paginate via the `X-Pagination-*` response headers when **Return All** is enabled.

Reference: [Assinafy v1 API documentation](https://api.assinafy.com.br/v1/docs) and [OpenAPI JSON](https://api.assinafy.com.br/v1/docs/openapi.json).

## How to read payloads

Unless a section says otherwise, the HTTP response is a JSON envelope:

```json
{
	"status": 200,
	"message": "",
	"data": "<operation payload>"
}
```

The node unwraps `data`, so each **Example response** below is the n8n JSON output, not the outer transport envelope. List operations emit one n8n item per resource unless the section documents a synthesized wrapper. A mutation that returns an array emits one item shaped as `{ "data": [...] }`. Empty delete responses may be converted to an explicit confirmation such as `{ "deleted": true }`.

Errors keep the API's standard shape and are surfaced by n8n as node errors:

```json
{
	"status": 400,
	"message": "Bad request.",
	"data": null
}
```

The API error families are validation, unauthorized, not found, and server errors. Account deletion can additionally return the complete blocker payload below. `PendingDocuments` appears only together with `ActivePaidSubscription`, never alone.

```json
{
	"status": 400,
	"message": "Cannot delete while restrictions are active.",
	"data": null,
	"restrictions": [
		{
			"code": "ActivePaidSubscription",
			"message": "Account has an active paid subscription.",
			"account_ids": ["<workspace-id>"]
		}
	]
}
```

Requests have a 30-second timeout. HTTP 429 responses are retried at most three times, honoring `Retry-After` up to 30 seconds and otherwise using bounded exponential backoff. When the retries are exhausted, the error description lists the response's `X-Rate-Limit-Limit`, `X-Rate-Limit-Remaining`, `X-Rate-Limit-Reset` and `Retry-After` values. Other failures are not retried. Embedded-session creation and OAuth code exchange, refresh, and revocation are never automatically replayed, including after HTTP 429 and including the refresh n8n performs for the OAuth2 credential. Avoid workflow-level retries of single-use tokens or mutations whose outcome is unknown.

Binary endpoints do not use the JSON envelope in the node output. Their bytes are written to the configured n8n binary property and the JSON side contains only file metadata. Multipart endpoints describe their form parts rather than pretending they have a JSON request body.

The action-node credential is optional for public routes, signer-access-code flows, and calls that supply a Bearer token. When no credential is selected, those requests default to `https://api.assinafy.com.br/v1`. If a selected credential cannot be loaded, the node fails rather than redirecting the request or a Bearer token to production. Select a Sandbox credential to route even an unauthenticated request to `https://sandbox.assinafy.com.br/v1`. Account-scoped and API-key-authenticated operations still require a credential and its Account ID. Custom credential URLs must be absolute HTTPS URLs ending in `/v1` (HTTP is allowed only for loopback hosts) and cannot contain user info, a query, or a fragment; the same validation runs before credential Test and runtime authentication attach the API key. The trigger always requires a credential.

Examples use synthetic placeholders only:

- `<account-id>`, `<workspace-id>`, `<document-id>`, `<signer-id>`, `<government-id>`, `<telephone>`, and similar values are identifiers.
- `<signer-access-code>`, `<verification-code>`, `<access-token>`, `<api-key>`, password, reset-token, and provider-token placeholders represent secrets that must never be logged or committed.
- All example email addresses use the reserved `example.com` domain.

GET requests and bodyless DELETE operations send no request body unless their section explicitly specifies one. Authenticated requests use `Accept: application/json` and either `X-Api-Key: <api-key>` or `Authorization: Bearer <oauth-access-token>`; JSON bodies add `Content-Type: application/json`. Multipart content types and boundaries are generated by the HTTP client. The endpoint paths below are relative to `/v1` unless an absolute URL is shown. With Return All enabled, requests add `page` starting at 1 and `per-page=100`; without it, Limit defaults to 50 and is clamped to 1–100. The server may cap pages at 50. Pagination uses page-count headers when present, otherwise the returned per-page header (or a conservative 50-record fallback), so a capped page is not mistaken for the end. Other list endpoints describe their own output wrapper. OAuth flat responses are documented separately.

## Shared schema catalog

The following OpenAPI schemas define complete payloads, nested fields, required properties, nullability and allowed values. `$ref` resolves to the named schema in this catalog. `Envelope` is the HTTP wrapper; `DocumentEnvelope` is a group of signing documents. The node unwraps HTTP `data`, preserves unknown fields and wraps array-valued mutations as `{ "data": [...] }`. Examples in operation sections illustrate payloads; these schemas define their full shape.

#### Schema: Envelope

```json
{
  "description": "Standard success wrapper. Operations add their own `data`.",
  "properties": {
    "status": {
      "description": "HTTP status code, mirrored in the body.",
      "type": "integer"
    },
    "message": {
      "description": "Human-readable message; empty on success.",
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  "type": "object"
}
```

#### Schema: ErrorEnvelope

```json
{
  "description": "Standard error wrapper. `status` mirrors the HTTP status code.",
  "properties": {
    "status": {
      "type": "integer"
    },
    "message": {
      "description": "Human-readable error message.",
      "type": "string"
    },
    "data": {
      "type": [
        "object",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "code": {
      "description": "On the refusals a client can act on: see Reading a refusal. Other refusals have only `message`.",
      "type": "string"
    },
    "errors": {
      "description": "On a `400` about a field (a model's validation, a missing or an unknown field): one entry per field in error; `message` stays the first entry's. Other `400`s have only `message`. See Reading a refusal.",
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "required": [
          "field",
          "code",
          "message"
        ],
        "properties": {
          "field": {
            "description": "The field as sent, a dot for a nested one (`address.line1`); `null` when the request as a whole is refused.",
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ]
          },
          "code": {
            "description": "`Required`, `UnknownField` or `Invalid`; the contract endpoints have codes of their own.",
            "type": "string"
          },
          "message": {
            "description": "In the platform's language.",
            "type": "string"
          }
        },
        "type": "object"
      }
    }
  },
  "type": "object"
}
```

#### Schema: Signer

```json
{
  "description": "A signing party belonging to a workspace account.",
  "properties": {
    "resource": {
      "description": "Present in single-resource responses.",
      "type": "string"
    },
    "id": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "full_name": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "email": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ],
      "format": "email"
    },
    "whatsapp_phone_number": {
      "description": "E.164 format; normalized on save.",
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "government_id": {
      "description": "Signer's normalized CPF (11 digits) or CNPJ (14 characters; may be alphanumeric).",
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "has_accepted_terms": {
      "type": "boolean"
    }
  },
  "type": "object"
}
```

#### Schema: SignerSelf

```json
{
  "description": "The current signer, as returned by `GET /v1/signers/self`. Extends Signer with the signature-state flags that are only computed for the authenticated signer.",
  "type": "object",
  "allOf": [
    {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Signer"
    },
    {
      "properties": {
        "has_signature": {
          "description": "Whether the signer has a saved signature image stored.",
          "type": "boolean"
        },
        "has_initial": {
          "description": "Whether the signer has a saved initials image stored.",
          "type": "boolean"
        },
        "is_signature_reusable": {
          "description": "Whether the signer opted to reuse their saved signature/initials in future processes. When false, clients should not pre-render the saved image even if `has_signature`/`has_initial` is true.",
          "type": "boolean"
        },
        "verification_method": {
          "description": "How this signature request verifies the signer. `Email`/`Whatsapp` ask for the 6-digit code; `IdDocumentFaceMatchBasic` runs the identity document check instead (see **Identity document verification status**).",
          "type": "string",
          "enum": [
            "Email",
            "Whatsapp",
            "DigitalCertificate",
            "IdDocumentFaceMatchBasic"
          ]
        }
      },
      "type": "object"
    }
  ]
}
```

#### Schema: DisplaySettings

```json
{
  "description": "A field placement rectangle on a document page. Geometry values are pixels in Assinafy's 150-DPI page image, measured from the upper-left corner. Clients must keep the rectangle within the selected page's width and height; the API does not clamp out-of-bounds values.",
  "required": [
    "left",
    "top",
    "width",
    "height",
    "fontSize"
  ],
  "properties": {
    "left": {
      "description": "Horizontal distance from the page's left edge, in page-image pixels.",
      "type": "number",
      "format": "float",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "top": {
      "description": "Vertical distance from the page's top edge, in page-image pixels.",
      "type": "number",
      "format": "float",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "width": {
      "description": "Width of the placement rectangle, in page-image pixels.",
      "type": "number",
      "format": "float",
      "exclusiveMinimum": 0
    },
    "height": {
      "description": "Height of the placement rectangle, in page-image pixels.",
      "type": "number",
      "format": "float",
      "exclusiveMinimum": 0
    },
    "fontFamily": {
      "description": "Font-family presentation metadata.",
      "type": "string"
    },
    "fontSize": {
      "description": "Font size in the 150-DPI page-image coordinate system.",
      "type": "number",
      "format": "float",
      "exclusiveMinimum": 0
    },
    "backgroundColor": {
      "description": "CSS-compatible background-color presentation metadata.",
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  "type": "object"
}
```


#### Schema: Document

```json
{
  "description": "A document and its current lifecycle state.",
  "properties": {
    "resource": {
      "description": "Present in single-resource responses.",
      "type": "string"
    },
    "id": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "account_id": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "template_id": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "status": {
      "description": "Status code — see GET /v1/documents/statuses.",
      "type": "string"
    },
    "artifacts": {
      "description": "Artifact download URLs keyed by name. Always `original`, plus `thumbnail` once one exists. A certificated document also carries `certificated`, `certificate-page` and `bundle`, and `pades` when it was signed with a digital certificate — the PAdES version holds the signers' ICP-Brasil signatures, which certification flattens out of the certificated PDF.",
      "type": "object"
    },
    "is_closed": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "signing_url": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "verification_code": {
      "description": "The code printed on the certificate page. Anyone can check it with `GET /v1/documents/{verification_code}/verify` or on `verification_url`. `null` until the document is `certificated`, and on documents certificated before verification codes existed.",
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "verification_url": {
      "description": "The public verification page for this document. `null` until the document is `certificated`.",
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ],
      "format": "uri"
    },
    "decline_reason": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "declined_by": {
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Signer"
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    },
    "tags": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "properties": {
          "id": {
            "type": "string"
          },
          "name": {
            "type": "string"
          },
          "color": {
            "description": "6-char hex without leading #.",
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ]
          }
        },
        "type": "object"
      }
    },
    "assignment": {
      "description": "The document's assignment, or `null` before one is created. The server decides when it is included, whatever `expand` the request sends: the document list, `GET /v1/documents/{documentId}`, the signer's document list and document creation from a template always include it; `/documents/search` and the upload response never do. Only the rename response follows `?expand=assignment`.",
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Assignment"
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    },
    "pages": {
      "description": "The document's pages. Included on the same responses as `assignment`, and also on the upload response.",
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/DocumentPage"
      }
    },
    "envelope_id": {
      "description": "Only on `GET /v1/documents/{documentId}?expand=envelope_id` read by the workspace, not in document lists or webhooks, so their payloads keep their shape: the envelope this document is in, or `null` for a document sent on its own, taken out of its envelope, or whose envelope was deleted.",
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "envelope": {
      "description": "Only on `GET /v1/documents/{documentId}?expand=envelope` read by the workspace and `GET /v1/sign?expand=envelope` or `GET /v1/signers/{signerId}/document?expand=envelope` read with the signer's code, not in document lists or webhooks: the envelope this document is in, or `null` for a document sent on its own, taken out of its envelope, or whose envelope was deleted. The signer's reads name the envelope their code belongs to even when the code's own document was taken out, since the code still reaches the rest.",
      "required": [
        "id",
        "name"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "id": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "name": {
          "type": "string"
        }
      },
      "type": [
        "object",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "has_contract": {
      "description": "Only on `GET /v1/documents/{documentId}` read by the workspace, while the environment has contract tracking on: not with a signer's access code, not in document lists or webhooks, and left out when the lookup fails, so a missing key means unknown. Whether `GET /v1/accounts/{accountId}/documents/{documentId}/contract` answers with contract terms, the document's own or those of a contract it amends whose document still exists, so a page can lay out the contract section before reading it.",
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "created_at": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "date-time"
    },
    "updated_at": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "date-time"
    }
  },
  "type": "object"
}
```

#### Schema: SigningDocument

```json
{
  "description": "The document as its signer reads it with the access code: the document with `current_signer`, `is_signer_access_closed` and `certified_copy`. Once the code is closed, only `id`, `name`, `status` and these three, and `envelope` when asked for.",
  "required": [
    "id",
    "name",
    "status",
    "current_signer",
    "is_signer_access_closed",
    "certified_copy"
  ],
  "type": "object",
  "allOf": [
    {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Document"
    },
    {
      "properties": {
        "current_signer": {
          "description": "This signer. Once the code is closed, only `id` and the last three.",
          "type": "object",
          "allOf": [
            {
              "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Signer"
            },
            {
              "properties": {
                "verification_method": {
                  "description": "This signature request's verification method.",
                  "type": "string"
                },
                "notification_methods": {
                  "description": "This signature request's notification methods; `null` on requests stored before they were recorded.",
                  "type": [
                    "array",
                    "null"
                  ],
                  "items": {
                    "type": "string"
                  }
                },
                "completed": {
                  "description": "Whether this signer has signed.",
                  "type": "boolean"
                }
              },
              "type": "object"
            }
          ]
        },
        "is_signer_access_closed": {
          "description": "Whether the code is closed.",
          "type": "boolean"
        },
        "certified_copy": {
          "description": "Whether the signer can ask for the certified copy again (`POST /v1/sign/certified-copy`).",
          "required": [
            "available",
            "notification_method",
            "resends_left"
          ],
          "properties": {
            "available": {
              "type": "boolean"
            },
            "notification_method": {
              "description": "The channel it goes on; `null` when there is none.",
              "type": [
                "string",
                "null"
              ],
              "enum": [
                "Email",
                "Whatsapp",
                null
              ]
            },
            "resends_left": {
              "description": "WhatsApp re-sends left; `null` for e-mail.",
              "type": [
                "integer",
                "null"
              ]
            }
          },
          "type": "object"
        }
      },
      "type": "object"
    }
  ]
}
```

#### Schema: Account

```json
{
  "description": "A workspace account (organization).",
  "properties": {
    "resource": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "id": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "primary_color": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "secondary_color": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "notification_sender_type": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "User",
        "Account"
      ]
    },
    "roles": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string"
      }
    },
    "is_delete_allowed": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "limits": {
      "description": "Limits of the workspace's current plan. Returned only by `GET /v1/accounts/{accountId}`, not in account lists. More keys may be added.",
      "properties": {
        "webhook_endpoints": {
          "description": "How many webhook endpoints the workspace can have: `1`, or `3` with the Multiple Webhooks feature. Creating one past the limit returns `403`.",
          "type": "integer"
        }
      },
      "type": "object"
    },
    "signing_features": {
      "description": "Only with `?expand=signing_features` on `GET /v1/accounts/{accountId}`: which signing features the workspace's subscription includes now (its plan's and any granted to it), so a client offers only the identity checks and notices it can use. `DigitalCertificate` and `VerificationIdDocumentFaceMatchBasic` gate the `DigitalCertificate` and `IdDocumentFaceMatchBasic` verification methods, `WhatsappNotification` the `Whatsapp` notification method (and so the `Whatsapp` verification method), and `VerificationBypass` the `Bypass` verification method. `false` means creating an assignment, or a document from a template, with that method (or estimating its cost) answers `403`. `Embed` gates embedded signing: with `false`, the embed origins endpoints, `\"embedded\": true` on a signer and minting an embed session answer `403` with code `PlanRestricted`. `true` is not a promise: credits, the assignment's own rules (a `DigitalCertificate` signer needs a `virtual` assignment) and a change of plan still apply. Requires the `account:read` OAuth scope.",
      "required": [
        "DigitalCertificate",
        "VerificationIdDocumentFaceMatchBasic",
        "WhatsappNotification",
        "VerificationBypass",
        "Embed"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "DigitalCertificate": {
          "description": "Signing with the signer's own ICP-Brasil certificate.",
          "type": "boolean"
        },
        "VerificationIdDocumentFaceMatchBasic": {
          "description": "Identity check by ID document and face match.",
          "type": "boolean"
        },
        "WhatsappNotification": {
          "description": "Sending signature requests by WhatsApp.",
          "type": "boolean"
        },
        "VerificationBypass": {
          "description": "Signing without a verification code (the `Bypass` verification method).",
          "type": "boolean"
        },
        "Embed": {
          "description": "Embedded signing: the signing screen inside your own app (see **Embedded Signing Guide**).",
          "type": "boolean"
        }
      },
      "type": "object"
    },
    "created_at": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "date-time"
    }
  },
  "type": "object"
}
```

#### Schema: DocumentContract

```json
{
  "description": "The contract terms tracked on a document. Every key below is always present, `null` when it holds no value, except `resource`, `document` and `amendments`, which depend on the response.",
  "required": [
    "document_id",
    "status",
    "counterparty_name",
    "counterparty_taxpayer_id",
    "value",
    "currency",
    "start_date",
    "end_date",
    "auto_renew",
    "renewal_term_months",
    "notice_period_days",
    "term",
    "owner_id",
    "metadata",
    "external_references",
    "created_at",
    "updated_at"
  ],
  "properties": {
    "resource": {
      "description": "On a response that is this contract alone; list items carry none.",
      "type": "string"
    },
    "document_id": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "status": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "draft",
        "active",
        "expired",
        "terminated"
      ]
    },
    "counterparty_name": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "counterparty_taxpayer_id": {
      "description": "CPF or CNPJ, digits only.",
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "value": {
      "description": "Two decimal places at most.",
      "type": [
        "number",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "currency": {
      "description": "ISO 4217 code; `BRL` when a value is sent without one.",
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "start_date": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ],
      "format": "date"
    },
    "end_date": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ],
      "format": "date"
    },
    "auto_renew": {
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "renewal_term_months": {
      "type": [
        "integer",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "notice_period_days": {
      "type": [
        "integer",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "term": {
      "description": "Steps on every renewal, starting at 1.",
      "type": "integer"
    },
    "owner_id": {
      "description": "ID of the workspace member who owns the contract. When the owner is no longer a member of the workspace (removed, left, or deleted with their account), the hourly run hands the contract to the first owner who is still an active member, or leaves it without one; an inactive member stays the owner. The contract's e-mails (renewal notice, expiring, expired, renewed) go to the first of the owner and the document's creator who is an active member, as that member's notification preferences allow.",
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "metadata": {
      "description": "Up to 50 keys of your own, each holding text of at most 500 characters; `null` when there are none. Values come back exactly as sent. A key ending in `_at`, or `stuck_since`, cannot hold a number.",
      "type": [
        "object",
        "null"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": {
        "type": "string"
      }
    },
    "external_references": {
      "description": "IDs outside systems use for this contract, ordered by system and id; `[]` when there are none.",
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "required": [
          "system",
          "id"
        ],
        "properties": {
          "system": {
            "type": "string"
          },
          "id": {
            "type": "string"
          }
        },
        "type": "object"
      }
    },
    "document": {
      "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Document",
      "description": "Only with `expand=document`: the document the terms are tracked on."
    },
    "amendments": {
      "description": "Only on the contract read and the related read (`GET …/contract`, `GET …/contract/related`), never on the list or on the answer to a write, renewal or termination: the documents that amend, renew or terminate this contract, the applied ones first by `number`, then the pending ones.",
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/DocumentContractAmendment"
      }
    },
    "created_at": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "date-time"
    },
    "updated_at": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "date-time"
    }
  },
  "type": "object"
}
```

#### Schema: DocumentContractInput

```json
{
  "description": "Every attribute is optional and only the ones sent change. `null` clears an attribute, except `auto_renew` (back to `false`) and `currency` (back to `BRL` while a `value` is set); `status` does not accept `null`. A changed `end_date` starts a new `term`.",
  "properties": {
    "status": {
      "description": "On creation any status, so a contract that ended before it was tracked is registered as it ended: `expired` needs an `end_date` in the past, `terminated` its `end_date`; neither is walked by the lifecycle. Afterwards only `draft` or `active` (a contract in force never goes back to `draft`): `expired` and `terminated` are set by the lifecycle and the terminate endpoint; a closed contract is reopened by renewing it.",
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "draft",
        "active",
        "expired",
        "terminated"
      ]
    },
    "counterparty_name": {
      "description": "Emoji are kept; other characters outside the 3-byte range are dropped.",
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ],
      "maxLength": 255
    },
    "counterparty_taxpayer_id": {
      "description": "A valid CPF or CNPJ, formatted or not.",
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "value": {
      "description": "Two decimal places at most.",
      "type": [
        "number",
        "null"
      ],
      "maximum": 9999999999999.99,
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "currency": {
      "description": "Three letters, such as `BRL` or `USD`, stored in capitals; `BRL` when a `value` is set without one.",
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "start_date": {
      "description": "`YYYY-MM-DD`, from the year 1000.",
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ],
      "format": "date"
    },
    "end_date": {
      "description": "`YYYY-MM-DD`, on or after `start_date`.",
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ],
      "format": "date"
    },
    "auto_renew": {
      "description": "Whether the hourly run starts the next term the day after `end_date`, by `renewal_term_months`, instead of expiring the contract.",
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "renewal_term_months": {
      "description": "How far a renewal by the term moves `end_date`; 12 when unset.",
      "type": [
        "integer",
        "null"
      ],
      "maximum": 600,
      "minimum": 1
    },
    "notice_period_days": {
      "description": "Days before `end_date` the notice period starts (`document_contract_renewal_notice`); none when unset.",
      "type": [
        "integer",
        "null"
      ],
      "maximum": 3650,
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "owner_id": {
      "description": "An active member of this workspace.",
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "metadata": {
      "description": "Your own fields: up to 50 keys of 1 to 40 letters, digits, `_`, `-` or `.`, each holding plain text of at most 500 characters; no number under a key ending in `_at`, or `stuck_since`.",
      "maxProperties": 50,
      "type": [
        "object",
        "null"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": {
        "type": "string",
        "maxLength": 500
      }
    },
    "external_references": {
      "description": "Replaces the whole list; `[]` or `null` clears it. Two items whose `system` and `id` compare equal case aside are one reference (the first sent is kept); a pair the database takes for one beyond that (accents, `ß` and `ss`) answers `400`.",
      "type": [
        "array",
        "null"
      ],
      "items": {
        "required": [
          "system",
          "id"
        ],
        "properties": {
          "system": {
            "description": "Letters, digits, `_`, `-` and `.`; stored in lower case.",
            "type": "string",
            "maxLength": 64
          },
          "id": {
            "description": "Kept as sent; must not contain emoji or other 4-byte characters.",
            "type": "string",
            "maxLength": 255
          }
        },
        "type": "object"
      },
      "maxItems": 20
    },
    "parent_document_id": {
      "description": "Links the document as an amendment of this contract document, or of any document in its family (resolved to the root); `null` unlinks a pending amendment. With it, the other attributes are the terms the instrument changes, not terms of its own; on a pending amendment a `null` value drops that term from the delta rather than clearing it on the parent.",
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "relation_type": {
      "description": "Required on the first link. `amendment` may change `value`, `currency`, `start_date` (effective from, display only), `end_date`, `auto_renew`, `renewal_term_months`, `notice_period_days`, `counterparty_name`, `counterparty_taxpayer_id` and `metadata`; `renewal` only `end_date` and `value`; `termination` only `end_date` (the effective date).",
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "amendment",
        "renewal",
        "termination"
      ]
    },
    "updated_at": {
      "description": "Optional precondition: the `updated_at` the client read the contract (or this document's amendment link) with, as the API returned it. If the contract or link changed since, nothing is written and the answer is `409` with `code: ContractChanged`. Not sent, or `null`: the write applies whatever changed meanwhile (last write wins). Compared to the second; a fraction of a second, as a JavaScript date prints it, is read and ignored. New terms and a first link have nothing to have read, so sending one answers `409`; on a pending amendment, send the link's own `updated_at` (the GET on that document).",
      "type": "string",
      "format": "date-time"
    }
  },
  "type": "object"
}
```

#### Schema: DocumentContractAmendment

```json
{
  "description": "A document that amends, renews or terminates a contract: its link and the terms it changes. The parent's terms take the delta once this document is certificated; until then the parent is untouched. Every key below is always present, `null` when it holds no value, except `resource`.",
  "required": [
    "document_id",
    "document_name",
    "parent_document_id",
    "relation_type",
    "number",
    "status",
    "document_status",
    "apply_error",
    "currency",
    "terms",
    "changes",
    "applied_at",
    "created_at",
    "updated_at"
  ],
  "properties": {
    "resource": {
      "description": "On a response that is this amendment alone; the entries of a contract's `amendments` carry none.",
      "type": "string"
    },
    "document_id": {
      "description": "This document, the instrument.",
      "type": "string"
    },
    "document_name": {
      "description": "This document's name.",
      "type": "string"
    },
    "parent_document_id": {
      "description": "The contract document whose terms change.",
      "type": "string"
    },
    "relation_type": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "amendment",
        "renewal",
        "termination"
      ]
    },
    "number": {
      "description": "Position among the parent's applied amendments by signing date; `null` while pending.",
      "type": [
        "integer",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "status": {
      "description": "`applied` once the delta moved the parent's terms.",
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "pending",
        "applied"
      ]
    },
    "document_status": {
      "description": "This document's own status, so a declined, cancelled or expired instrument shows as such. An instrument applies only while its document is `certificated`: one whose document reads `failed` after it was signed waits, with no `apply_error`, until it is certificated again, and then applies in its place in the signing order.",
      "type": "string"
    },
    "apply_error": {
      "description": "Why a signed instrument has not applied yet, retried every hour: the workspace's plan does not include contract tracking (it applies on the first run after the plan returns), the parent would not take the delta, an instrument signed before this one has not applied yet, one signed after it has already applied (this one then stays blocked: unlink it, and record what it still changes on a new instrument), or an unexpected failure.",
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "currency": {
      "description": "The currency of the amounts in `terms` and `changes`. Once applied, the contract's currency at that moment, kept whatever the contract moves to later. While pending (and for an instrument applied before this was kept), the one this instrument moves the contract to, otherwise the contract's. `null` when neither has one.",
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "terms": {
      "description": "The delta: only the terms this instrument changes; `null` when it changes none (a termination on the day it is signed).",
      "type": [
        "object",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "changes": {
      "description": "Every parent field the roll-up moves, as `{from, to}`: a preview while pending, the record once applied; `null` when it moves none.",
      "type": [
        "object",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "applied_at": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ],
      "format": "date-time"
    },
    "created_at": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "date-time"
    },
    "updated_at": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "date-time"
    }
  },
  "type": "object"
}
```

#### Schema: SigningUrl

```json
{
  "description": "One entry per signer whose turn has come. The link depends on how the signer gets the invitation: with `Email` it is a page that does not open the document by itself, where the signer asks for their invitation again by e-mail; with `Whatsapp` only it is `null` (the signer has their link in the WhatsApp message). An embedded signer (`embedded: true`) never gets a link: `url` is `null` and the integrator mints an embed session for them instead.",
  "properties": {
    "signer_id": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "url": {
      "description": "See the schema description. `null` for a signer notified only by WhatsApp and for an embedded signer.",
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "embedded": {
      "description": "Whether the signer was marked `embedded` when the assignment was created.",
      "type": "boolean"
    }
  },
  "type": "object"
}
```

#### Schema: EnvelopeCreateRequest

```json
{
  "description": "An envelope as sent: on creation, and to estimate what creating it would cost.",
  "required": [
    "name",
    "signers",
    "documents"
  ],
  "properties": {
    "name": {
      "description": "What the invitation calls what is being signed, in place of a document's name. `<` is refused (`400`).",
      "type": "string",
      "maxLength": 255,
      "minLength": 1
    },
    "message": {
      "description": "Optional message sent to every signer; their invitation adds the names of the documents they are on.",
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "max_total_credits": {
      "description": "Optional ceiling on what this request may spend, as on an assignment: refused with `409` and code `CreditCeilingExceeded`, nothing created or charged, when the envelope's total credits are above it. A value that is not a number of credits, zero or more, answers `400`; numeric text counts, as a form-encoded body sends it. The estimate ignores it.",
      "type": "number",
      "minimum": 0
    },
    "signers": {
      "description": "Every signer of the envelope, once, by id: up to 20. They all sign at once: an envelope takes no `step`.",
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "required": [
          "id"
        ],
        "properties": {
          "id": {
            "description": "An existing signer's ID.",
            "type": "string",
            "minLength": 1
          },
          "verification_method": {
            "description": "As on an assignment. A `DigitalCertificate` signer signs only virtual documents, alone on each, each with their certificate, and is charged on each. An `IdDocumentFaceMatchBasic` signer checks their identity once for the whole envelope, and is charged for it once.",
            "type": "string",
            "enum": [
              "Email",
              "Whatsapp",
              "DigitalCertificate",
              "IdDocumentFaceMatchBasic"
            ]
          },
          "notification_methods": {
            "description": "As on an assignment: one method, inferred from `verification_method` when left out.",
            "type": "array",
            "items": {
              "type": "string",
              "enum": [
                "Email",
                "Whatsapp"
              ]
            },
            "maxItems": 1
          },
          "embedded": {
            "description": "As on an assignment: the signer signs inside your app, through an embed session, on every document of the envelope, the ones added later too. Their `signing_urls` entry has no link (`url` is `null`). An embed session minted on any of their documents still waiting for them lists the documents of the envelope they still have to sign, and the code their invitation carries verifies them there.",
            "type": "boolean",
            "default": false
          }
        },
        "type": "object"
      },
      "maxItems": 20,
      "minItems": 1
    },
    "documents": {
      "description": "The documents, in the order the envelope lists them: up to 20.",
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/EnvelopeDocumentRequest"
      },
      "maxItems": 20,
      "minItems": 1
    }
  },
  "type": "object"
}
```

#### Schema: EnvelopeDocumentRequest

```json
{
  "description": "A document of an envelope, as sent on creation (`documents[]`) and when added.",
  "required": [
    "id",
    "signer_ids"
  ],
  "properties": {
    "id": {
      "description": "A `metadata_ready` document of the workspace with no assignment.",
      "type": "string",
      "minLength": 1
    },
    "method": {
      "description": "As on an assignment: `virtual` (the default), signed on its virtual page, or `collect`, signed on the fields placed in `entries`.",
      "type": "string",
      "default": "virtual",
      "enum": [
        "virtual",
        "collect"
      ]
    },
    "entries": {
      "description": "Required for `collect`, as on an assignment: the fields placed on each page of the document. Its signers are the ones on its fields, exactly its `signer_ids`.",
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "required": [
          "page_id",
          "fields"
        ],
        "properties": {
          "page_id": {
            "description": "A page of the document.",
            "type": "string"
          },
          "fields": {
            "type": "array",
            "items": {
              "required": [
                "signer_id",
                "field_id",
                "display_settings"
              ],
              "properties": {
                "signer_id": {
                  "description": "A signer of the document.",
                  "type": "string"
                },
                "field_id": {
                  "description": "A field definition of the workspace.",
                  "type": "string"
                },
                "display_settings": {
                  "$ref": "#/components/schemas/DisplaySettings"
                }
              },
              "type": "object"
            },
            "minItems": 1
          }
        },
        "type": "object"
      },
      "minItems": 1
    },
    "signer_ids": {
      "description": "The signers of this document, each listed once: on creation, signers listed in `signers`; when added, signers of the envelope's documents.",
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string"
      },
      "minItems": 1
    },
    "expires_at": {
      "description": "This document's expiration, read as on an assignment, at least one hour in the future. Text is read as a date: ISO 8601 is the format to send (a UTC offset in the value is honored, a value without one is read as UTC, and a date alone means 23:59 UTC that day), and text that is a number is Unix time. A number is Unix time in seconds, its fraction dropped. Null, 0, an empty text or leaving it out means no expiration; a value that can't be read as a date answers `400`.",
      "oneOf": [
        {
          "type": "string"
        },
        {
          "type": "number"
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    },
    "copy_receiver_ids": {
      "description": "Signer IDs that only receive a copy of this document, as on an assignment.",
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "type": "string"
      }
    }
  },
  "type": "object"
}
```

#### Schema: DocumentEnvelope

```json
{
  "description": "Documents sent together to the same signers. Each document keeps its own assignment, status, certificate and downloads.",
  "required": [
    "id",
    "name",
    "message",
    "documents",
    "counts",
    "signing_urls",
    "created_at"
  ],
  "properties": {
    "resource": {
      "description": "Present in single-resource responses.",
      "type": "string"
    },
    "id": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "name": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "message": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "documents": {
      "description": "The envelope's documents, in the order it lists them.",
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "required": [
          "id",
          "position",
          "name",
          "status",
          "artifacts",
          "signers"
        ],
        "properties": {
          "id": {
            "type": "string"
          },
          "position": {
            "description": "The document's place in the envelope's order, from 1.",
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 1
          },
          "name": {
            "type": "string"
          },
          "status": {
            "description": "Status code — see GET /v1/documents/statuses.",
            "type": "string"
          },
          "artifacts": {
            "description": "The document's files, as its own read (`GET /v1/documents/{documentId}`) gives them: always `original`, plus `thumbnail` once one exists, and for a certificated document `certificated`, `certificate-page`, `bundle` and, when signed with a digital certificate, `pades`. In the envelope list (`GET /v1/accounts/{accountId}/envelopes`) only `thumbnail`, given without checking that it exists: its URL answers `404` until the thumbnail is generated.",
            "type": "object",
            "additionalProperties": {
              "type": "string"
            }
          },
          "signers": {
            "description": "The document's signers: a signer appears only on the documents they are on.",
            "type": "array",
            "items": {
              "required": [
                "id",
                "full_name",
                "email",
                "whatsapp_phone_number",
                "completed",
                "signed_at"
              ],
              "properties": {
                "id": {
                  "type": "string"
                },
                "full_name": {
                  "type": "string"
                },
                "email": {
                  "type": [
                    "string",
                    "null"
                  ]
                },
                "whatsapp_phone_number": {
                  "type": [
                    "string",
                    "null"
                  ]
                },
                "completed": {
                  "description": "Whether they have signed this document.",
                  "type": "boolean"
                },
                "signed_at": {
                  "description": "When they signed this document; `null` until they do.",
                  "type": [
                    "string",
                    "null"
                  ],
                  "format": "date-time"
                }
              },
              "type": "object"
            }
          }
        },
        "type": "object"
      }
    },
    "counts": {
      "description": "How many of its documents are in each status (status code => count); only the statuses present. An envelope has no status of its own. `null` once it lists no document: every one deleted or taken out.",
      "type": [
        "object",
        "null"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": {
        "type": "integer"
      }
    },
    "signing_urls": {
      "description": "One entry per signer, as on an assignment (`SigningUrl`): the link of the document their invitation is sent from while it waits for them, else of their first document that still does (the e-mail page asks that document for the invitation again; any of their links opens the whole envelope).",
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/SigningUrl"
      }
    },
    "created_at": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "date-time"
    }
  },
  "type": "object"
}
```

#### Schema: CostEstimate

```json
{
  "description": "Cost breakdown for an assignment plus current account balances. `credits` is the sum of `breakdown` (notifications, digital-certificate signatures and identity checks). `total_credits` is what the send will take from the credit balance: `credits`, plus `extra_document_cost` when `needs_extra_document` is true. The extra document is not a `breakdown` line.",
  "required": [
    "documents",
    "credits",
    "needs_extra_document",
    "extra_document_cost",
    "total_credits",
    "breakdown",
    "document_balance",
    "credit_balance",
    "has_sufficient_resources",
    "blocking_reason",
    "message"
  ],
  "properties": {
    "documents": {
      "description": "Documents consumed: 1, for an assignment as for an envelope, whatever its documents.",
      "type": "integer"
    },
    "credits": {
      "description": "Sum of the `breakdown` costs: notifications, digital-certificate signatures and identity checks. Does not include the extra document.",
      "type": "number"
    },
    "needs_extra_document": {
      "description": "True when the plan's document allowance is exhausted and an extra document will be charged from credits.",
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "extra_document_cost": {
      "description": "Credits for one extra document. Charged only when `needs_extra_document` is true.",
      "type": "number"
    },
    "total_credits": {
      "description": "Credits the send will consume: `credits`, plus `extra_document_cost` when `needs_extra_document` is true.",
      "type": "number"
    },
    "breakdown": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/CostEstimateBreakdownItem"
      }
    },
    "document_balance": {
      "description": "Documents left in the plan. `null` when the account's documents are not limited.",
      "type": [
        "number",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "credit_balance": {
      "description": "Credits available. `null` when the account's credits are not limited.",
      "type": [
        "number",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "has_sufficient_resources": {
      "description": "Whether creating it would pass its balance checks now: the checks creation itself makes, so a contract that allows overage passes with a low `document_balance` or `credit_balance`.",
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "blocking_reason": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ],
      "enum": [
        "PendingPayment",
        "InsufficientDocuments",
        "InsufficientCredits"
      ]
    },
    "message": {
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    }
  },
  "type": "object"
}
```

#### Schema: ResendCostEstimate

```json
{
  "description": "Credits a reminder to one signer will take, and whether the account can pay for it. A reminder uses no document.",
  "properties": {
    "total": {
      "description": "Sum of the `breakdown` costs.",
      "type": "number"
    },
    "breakdown": {
      "type": "array",
      "items": {
        "properties": {
          "code": {
            "type": "string"
          },
          "name": {
            "type": "string"
          },
          "cost": {
            "type": "number"
          }
        },
        "type": "object"
      }
    },
    "credit_balance": {
      "description": "Credits available. `null` when the account's credits are not limited.",
      "type": [
        "number",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "has_sufficient_credits": {
      "description": "Whether the reminder would pass its credit check now: the check the reminder itself makes, so a contract that allows overage passes with a low `credit_balance`, and a plan whose payment is pending doesn't, whatever the balance.",
      "type": "boolean"
    }
  },
  "type": "object"
}
```

```json
      "format": "email"

      }

    "is_active": {
      "description": "Whether events are delivered to this endpoint.",
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "is_held": {
      "description": "Whether deliveries are paused after 10 failures in a row: we probe the endpoint on a growing schedule and deliver what it missed when it answers (see **Delivery contract**). Editing the URL, or sending `is_active: true`, lifts the pause.",
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "held_since": {
      "description": "When the pause began (Unix timestamp, seconds); `null` while deliveries run.",
      "type": [
        "integer",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "signing_enabled": {
      "description": "Whether deliveries carry a `webhook-signature` header (see **Webhook Payloads → Verifying signatures**).",
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "created_at": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "date-time"
    },
    "updated_at": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "date-time"
    }
  },
  "type": "object"
}
```

#### Schema: WebhookDispatch

```json
{
  "description": "A single webhook delivery-history entry.",
  "properties": {
    "resource": {
      "description": "Always `activity_dispatching_history` in single-resource responses.",
      "type": "string"
    },
    "id": {
      "description": "Dispatch entry ID.",
      "type": "string"
    },
    "event": {
      "description": "Event type that triggered the dispatch.",
      "type": "string"
    },
    "activity_id": {
      "description": "Internal activity ID associated with the dispatch.",
      "type": "integer"
    },
    "endpoint_id": {
      "description": "ID of the webhook endpoint the delivery was sent to (`null` once that endpoint is deleted).",
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "endpoint": {
      "description": "URL that received the request.",
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "payload": {
      "description": "JSON payload sent to the endpoint.",
      "type": [
        "object",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "delivered": {
      "description": "Whether delivery succeeded.",
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "webhook_id": {
      "description": "The `webhook-id` header of the request: the same on every attempt of this event to this endpoint.",
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "attempt": {
      "description": "Which attempt of this event to this endpoint the entry records, counted from 1.",
      "type": [
        "integer",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "kind": {
      "description": "`scheduled`: the delivery of the event as it happened; `probe`: the one delivery we send to a paused endpoint to see whether it answers; `manual`: asked for through **Retry webhook delivery** or **Send a test delivery**.",
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "scheduled",
        "probe",
        "manual"
      ]
    },
    "will_retry": {
      "description": "Whether another attempt follows this one. `false` on a delivered entry and on the last attempt of a failed one.",
      "type": "boolean"
    },
    "duration_ms": {
      "description": "How long the endpoint took to answer or fail, in milliseconds. `null` when no request was sent (a host that does not resolve, an address that is not public) and on entries recorded before it was measured.",
      "type": [
        "integer",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "http_status": {
      "description": "HTTP status returned (null if connection failed).",
      "type": [
        "integer",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "response_body": {
      "description": "Endpoint response body, truncated to 2000 chars.",
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "error": {
      "description": "Delivery error message, if any.",
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "created_at": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "date-time"
    },
    "updated_at": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "date-time"
    }
  },
  "type": "object"
}
```

#### Schema: WebhookEvent

```json
{
  "description": "Body of every webhook delivery. Timestamps in the body (`created_at`, and the `*_at` fields of `subject`/`object`) are Unix timestamps in seconds, not the ISO strings the REST responses use. `subject` and `object` are serialized from their current state when the delivery is sent.",
  "required": [
    "id",
    "event",
    "created_at",
    "subject",
    "object",
    "account_id"
  ],
  "properties": {
    "id": {
      "description": "ID of the activity that produced the event.",
      "type": "integer"
    },
    "event": {
      "description": "Event type. See **List webhook event types**; `webhook_test` is sent only by **Send a test delivery**.",
      "type": "string"
    },
    "message": {
      "description": "Reserved; currently always `null`.",
      "type": [
        "string",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "payload": {
      "description": "Event-specific parameters; keys vary per event.",
      "type": [
        "object",
        "null"
      ],
      "additionalProperties": true
    },
    "origin": {
      "description": "Where the action came from, when it was triggered by a request.",
      "properties": {
        "ip": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "user-agent": {
          "type": "string"
        }
      },
      "type": [
        "object",
        "null"
      ]
    },
    "created_at": {
      "description": "When the event was recorded (Unix timestamp, seconds).",
      "type": "integer"
    },
    "subject": {
      "description": "Who performed the action: a `User`, `Signer` or `Account` (the endpoint itself in a `webhook_test`), plus a `type` property naming it.",
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": true
    },
    "object": {

}
```

#### Schema: OAuthTokenRequest

```json
{
  "description": "Body of `POST /v1/oauth/token`. Sent form-encoded per RFC 6749, or as JSON.",
  "required": [
    "grant_type",
    "client_id"
  ],
  "properties": {
    "grant_type": {
      "description": "`urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:token-exchange` is for internal service clients only (Assinafy's own MCP server) — an ordinary confidential or public client authenticates with it and always gets `invalid_client`, exactly as an unrecognized client would. Everyday integrators use `authorization_code` and `refresh_token`.",
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "authorization_code",
        "refresh_token",
        "urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:token-exchange"
      ]
    },
    "code": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "redirect_uri": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uri"
    },
    "code_verifier": {
      "description": "RFC 7636: 43-128 characters from [A-Za-z0-9-._~]. Shorter values are rejected with `invalid_grant`.",
      "type": "string"
    },
    "refresh_token": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "client_id": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "client_secret": {
      "description": "Confidential clients only. Public clients authenticate with PKCE and are never issued a secret; the token-exchange grant requires a confidential, internal-service client and therefore always requires this.",
      "type": "string"
    },
    "resource": {
      "description": "RFC 8707 resource indicator. For `authorization_code`/`refresh_token`, optional; when present it must be the `resource` value published by /.well-known/oauth-protected-resource and must match the one sent to /authorize, otherwise `invalid_target`. For the token-exchange grant it is REQUIRED and must equal this API's own resource identifier exactly (never a front-end resource such as the MCP server), otherwise `invalid_target`.",
      "type": "string",
      "format": "uri"
    },
    "subject_token": {
      "description": "Token-exchange grant only. The front-end resource's access token being traded in. Must be a live, original (never itself exchanged) token minted for a resource this server issues tokens for, other than this API's own audience.",
      "type": "string"
    },
    "subject_token_type": {
      "description": "Token-exchange grant only. Required; only `urn:ietf:params:oauth:token-type:access_token` is supported.",
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "urn:ietf:params:oauth:token-type:access_token"
      ]
    },
    "requested_token_type": {
      "description": "Token-exchange grant only. Optional; when present it must agree with the only type this server issues.",
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "urn:ietf:params:oauth:token-type:access_token"
      ]
    }
  },
  "type": "object"
}
```

#### Schema: OAuthRevokeRequest

```json
{
  "description": "Body of `POST /v1/oauth/revoke`. Sent form-encoded per RFC 7009, or as JSON.",
  "required": [
    "token",
    "client_id"
  ],
  "properties": {
    "token": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "token_type_hint": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "access_token",
        "refresh_token"
      ]
    },
    "client_id": {
      "type": "string"
    },
    "client_secret": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  "type": "object"
}
```

`DisplaySettings` uses pixels in the document’s 150-DPI page image, measured from the upper-left corner. `left` and `top` must be non-negative; `width`, `height` and `fontSize` must be positive. These five numeric fields are required for a placement. Keep each rectangle within the selected page’s width and height; the API does not clamp out-of-bounds coordinates. `fontFamily` and `backgroundColor` are optional presentation metadata.

`Document.verification_code` identifies the public verification page, and `verification_url` links to it; either can be null on older documents. `has_contract` is returned only by the workspace-authenticated single-document read when contract tracking is enabled: true means the document has terms or belongs to a family with terms. An omitted value is unknown, including when the lookup failed; lists, webhooks and signer-code reads omit it. Document List, Get, signer-side List and Create From Template include `assignment` and `pages`; Search omits both, Upload includes pages but no assignment, and Rename can include assignment through its expansion option.

`DocumentActivity.payload` is the event snapshot, with the same event-specific keys as its webhook. REST activity timestamps, including `*_at` keys inside payloads, are ISO 8601 strings; webhook body timestamps are Unix seconds.

`DocumentContract` and `DocumentContractAmendment` are documented field by field under [Contract schemas](#contract-schemas).

`SignerIdentityReview` is documented in full under [Get Identity Review](#get-identity-review), including every field, nullability and lifecycle state.

## Cross-operation behavior

Signer terms acceptance and OTP verification send `signer-access-code` in the query string. The OTP itself remains in the JSON body as `verification-code`. Field validation uses the explicit Authenticate With selection: Workspace Credentials (default) sends the selected credential; Signer Access Code sends only the required code. The Account ID still comes from the configured credential.

### Where a signer access code comes from

Signer-side operations authenticate with `signer-access-code` instead of the workspace API key. **In production, no endpoint returns that code.** It reaches the signer only out of band, in the body of the notification the API sends when an assignment is created (or resent) — by email, or by WhatsApp for a `Whatsapp` signer. Production provides no API-key-authenticated way to read, mint or look one up: holding one is equivalent to being that signer. In sandbox, List WhatsApp Notifications returns simulated button URLs containing the actual test codes; production strips those URLs.

In particular, the `signing_urls` array on an Assignment is **not** a source of access codes. Each entry addresses the web signing page for the _document_ and identifies the signer by email address:

```json
{
	"signer_id": "<signer-id>",
	"url": "https://app.assinafy.com.br/sign/<document-id>?email=signer%40example.com"
}
```

The path segment is the document ID, not a code — passing it as `signer-access-code` returns `401 Credenciais inválidas.` Give that URL to the signer and let the signing page do the rest, or send them there with **Document → Send Public Token** (`PUT /public/documents/{documentId}/send-token`), which mails a fresh one-time token.

An n8n workflow therefore drives the signer-side operations only when a code arrives through a step you control — an inbox-reading node, a WhatsApp integration, or a human pasting it in. Use the API-key credential for owner administration, and the OAuth credential for operations permitted by its scopes. See [OAuth](#oauth) for token-specific requirements.

### Verification, notification, and digital-certificate rules

Assignment and template signer rows use `verification_method` (`Email`, `Whatsapp`, `DigitalCertificate`, or `IdDocumentFaceMatchBasic`) and `notification_methods` (`Email` or `Whatsapp`). If both are omitted, both default to `Email`. Create and cost-estimate operations accept exactly one compatible notification channel per signer when supplied; the array shape is historical. When only a verification method is supplied, the API infers its notification channel. Create From Template also infers the verification method when only a notification channel is supplied.

| Verification               | Requirements                                                                                                                                     | Per-signer signature cost                |
| -------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------- |
| `Email`                    | Signer has an email address.                                                                                                                     | 0 credits                                |
| `Whatsapp`                 | Signer has `whatsapp_phone_number`; paid subscription.                                                                                           | 0 credits                                |
| `DigitalCertificate`       | `method: virtual`, Digital Certificate feature (Standard/Pro), signer CPF/CNPJ in `government_id`, and no other signer in the same signing step. | Per-signer fee returned by Estimate Cost |
| `IdDocumentFaceMatchBasic` | ID Document + Face Match feature, valid signer CPF in `government_id`, and explicit consent to biometric processing.                             | Per-signer fee returned by Estimate Cost |

Email notifications cost 0 credits. WhatsApp notifications require a paid subscription and cost 0.45 credits per notification.

`Email` verification pairs only with Email notification, and `Whatsapp` only with Whatsapp. `DigitalCertificate` and `IdDocumentFaceMatchBasic` accept either channel. Always check the returned cost estimate and `has_sufficient_resources` before creation; fees and plan entitlements are enforced by the API.

Biometric verification opens a hosted capture session after the signer consents. Open the returned URL unchanged, including any fragment, and keep it out of logs and public storage. A Pending session must be reused. Only Expired sessions can be restarted; Declined or Abandoned requires a new document and assignment. InReview lets the signer continue viewing, confirming data and signing, but certification waits for account approval. Once all signers finish, the document remains `pending_identity_review` until all reviews are approved; rejecting one cancels the document and notifies signers and copy receivers. Successful completion still requires `certificated` status and the certified artifact.

For `DigitalCertificate`, a CPF requires that person's e-CPF or an e-CNPJ naming that person as legal representative. A CNPJ requires an e-CNPJ for that company from any representative. Signing is completed through Assinafy's ICP-Brasil A1/A3 browser flow, which completes the signer's virtual assignment item; the ordinary signer **Sign** operation used for placed fields refuses certificate signers. Therefore `DigitalCertificate` requires `method: virtual`. Assignment Create and Estimate Cost reject `collect` before the request with stable error code `ASSINAFY_DIGITAL_CERTIFICATE_REQUIRES_VIRTUAL`. Template operations let the API resolve template roles and validate the resulting assignment before charging: Editor fields are baked into the document and do not make it `collect`; only placed Signer fields do. CopyReceiver roles do not sign and their verification, notification and step settings do not constrain signer steps. The certificate flow produces the optional `pades` artifact. The cost-estimate response reports the signature charge with breakdown code `SignatureDigitalCertificate`; an extra document costs 1 credit when the plan allowance is exhausted.

Assignment Create rejects multiple notification channels before sending. Template creation and estimation forward these settings for role-aware API validation; the API applies signing rules only after resolving Signer, CopyReceiver and Editor roles.

### Document

#### Upload

Uploads a new PDF from an incoming binary item and creates a document in the workspace.

**Endpoint:** `POST /accounts/{accountId}/documents`

**Node parameters:**

- Binary Property — string — required — name of the binary property holding the PDF (default `data`).
- File Name — string — optional — name sent to Assinafy; defaults to the binary file name, or `document.pdf`. Must end in `.pdf`.

The node enforces PDF-only, non-empty, and the 25MB upload limit before sending. The API additionally limits a document to 2,000 pages. The node checks the PDF file signature as well as any specific declared MIME type; a missing or generic `application/octet-stream` MIME type is accepted only when the bytes identify an allowed PDF. The request contains one `file` part whose multipart filename is the configured File Name.

**Example request:**

```
multipart/form-data:
  file: <binary PDF>   (filename = File Name)
```

**Example response:**

```json
{
	"resource": "document",
	"id": "1031abf...",
	"account_id": "<account-id>",
	"template_id": null,
	"name": "contract.pdf",
	"status": "uploaded",
	"artifacts": {
		"original": "https://.../documents/1031abf.../download/original"
	},
	"is_closed": false,
	"signing_url": "https://app.assinafy.com.br/sign/1031abf...",
	"decline_reason": null,
	"declined_by": null,
	"tags": [],
	"assignment": null,
	"pages": [],
	"created_at": "2026-06-05T12:00:00Z",
	"updated_at": "2026-06-05T12:00:00Z"
}
```

#### List workspace documents

Lists documents in the workspace, with optional filters; paginates via `X-Pagination-*` headers when Return All is set.

**Endpoint:** `GET /accounts/{accountId}/documents`

**Node parameters:**

- Return All — boolean — optional — return every page of results.
- Limit — number — optional — max results when Return All is off.
- Filters > Search — string — optional — partial match on document name, signer full name, signer email.
- Filters > Status — options — optional — filter by document status (e.g. `pending_signature`, `certificated`).
- Filters > Signature Method — options — optional — `virtual` or `collect`.
- Filters > Sort — options — optional — sort by `name` or `updated_at`.
- Filters > Tag IDs — string (multiple) — optional — tag IDs; returns documents having all listed tags (AND, comma-joined into `tags`).

**Example request:**

```json
{
	"search": "contract",
	"status": "pending_signature",
	"method": "virtual",
	"sort": "updated_at",
	"tags": "tagId1,tagId2"
}
```

**Example response:**

```json
[
	{
		"resource": "document",
		"id": "1031...",
		"account_id": "<account-id>",
		"template_id": null,
		"name": "document.pdf",
		"status": "metadata_ready",
		"artifacts": {
			"original": "https://.../download/original",
			"thumbnail": "https://.../thumbnail"
		},
		"is_closed": false,
		"signing_url": "https://app.assinafy.com.br/sign/1031...",
		"decline_reason": null,
		"declined_by": null,
		"tags": [],
		"assignment": null,
		"pages": [
			{
				"id": "...",
				"number": 1,
				"height": 417,
				"width": 417,
				"download_url": "https://.../pages/<pid>/download"
			}
		],
		"created_at": "2026-05-12T18:05:11Z",
		"updated_at": "2026-05-12T18:05:11Z"
	}
]
```

#### Get a document

Fetches a single document by ID, including expanded `assignment` and `pages`.

**Endpoint:** `GET /documents/{documentId}`

**Node parameters:**

- Document ID — resourceLocator — required — the document to fetch.

`decline_reason` is returned only when the credential belongs to the document creator.

**Example response:**

```json
{
	"resource": "document",
	"id": "<document-id-1>",
	"account_id": "<account-id>",
	"name": "3.pdf",
	"status": "pending_signature",
	"assignment": {
		"id": "<assignment-id-1>",
		"sender_email": "sender@example.com",
		"method": "virtual",
		"expires_at": null,
		"message": null,
		"signers": [
			{
				"id": "customid1",
				"full_name": "Signer 1",
				"email": "signer1@example.com",
				"whatsapp_phone_number": null,
				"has_accepted_terms": false,
				"completed": false,
				"notification_history": [],
				"verification_method": "Email",
				"notification_methods": ["Email"],
				"step": 1,
				"notified": true
			}
		],
		"copy_receivers": [],
		"items": [],
		"summary": {
			"signer_count": 1,
			"completed_count": 0,
			"signers": [{ "id": "customid1", "full_name": "Signer 1", "completed": false }]
		},
		"signing_urls": [{ "signer_id": "customid1", "url": "https://.../sign/..." }]
	},
	"tags": [],
	"pages": [],
	"is_closed": false,
	"created_at": "2026-05-12T18:05:11Z",
	"updated_at": "2026-05-12T18:05:11Z"
}
```

#### Rename a document

Changes a document's display name.

**Endpoint:** `PATCH /documents/{documentId}`

**Node parameters:**

- Document ID — resourceLocator — required — the document to rename.
- New Name — string — required — the new display name.

> Rename is allowed only while status is `uploaded` or `metadata_ready` and before an assignment exists. The API accepts at most 255 characters, removes diacritics, and replaces unsupported characters with dashes. If you rename immediately after upload, run **Wait Until Ready** first.

**Example request:**

```json
{ "name": "signed-contract.pdf" }
```

**Example response:**

```json
{
	"resource": "document",
	"id": "<document-id-1>",
	"account_id": "<account-id>",
	"template_id": null,
	"name": "signed-contract.pdf",
	"status": "metadata_ready",
	"artifacts": {
		"original": "https://.../download/original",
		"thumbnail": "https://.../thumbnail"
	},
	"is_closed": false,
	"tags": [],
	"created_at": "2026-07-20T18:49:23Z",
	"updated_at": "2026-07-20T19:00:00Z"
}
```

#### Delete a document

Deletes a document by ID. The SDK returns a synthesized confirmation rather than the API's empty `data` array.

**Endpoint:** `DELETE /documents/{documentId}`

**Request body:** none.

**Node parameters:**

- Document ID — resourceLocator — required — the document to delete.

> A document can only be deleted from a deletable status (see **List Statuses** for the `deletable` flag per status).

**Example response:**

```json
{ "deleted": true, "documentId": "<document-id-1>" }
```

#### Search documents (lightweight)

Searches workspace documents via the dedicated lightweight search endpoint. Unlike **List**, each record omits the expanded `pages`/`assignment` objects, so it is faster for name/status lookups. Honors **Return All** / **Limit** and paginates via `X-Pagination-*` headers.

**Endpoint:** `GET /accounts/{accountId}/documents/search`

**Node parameters:**

- Return All — boolean — return every page (default false).
- Limit — number — max records when Return All is off (default 50).
- Search Filters > Search — string — partial match on the document name.
- Search Filters > Status — string — filter by status code (e.g. `metadata_ready`, `pending_signature`).
- Search Filters > Sort — string — sort field, with `-` for descending (for example `-created_at`).

**Example request (query):**

```json
{
	"search": "contract",
	"status": "metadata_ready"
}
```

**Example response (one item per document):**

```json
[
	{
		"id": "<document-id-2>",
		"account_id": "<account-id>",
		"template_id": null,
		"name": "contract.pdf",
		"status": "metadata_ready",
		"artifacts": {
			"original": "https://.../download/original",
			"thumbnail": "https://.../thumbnail"
		},
		"is_closed": false,
		"signing_url": "https://app.assinafy.com.br/sign/<document-id-2>",
		"decline_reason": null,
		"declined_by": null,
		"tags": [],
		"created_at": "2026-07-20T18:49:23Z",
		"updated_at": "2026-07-20T18:49:26Z"
	}
]
```

#### Download a document artifact (PDF or ZIP)

Downloads a document artifact (`original`, certificated/signed PDF, certificate page, PAdES PDF, or ZIP bundle) into a binary property.

**Endpoint:** `GET /documents/{documentId}/download/{artifact}`

**Node parameters:**

- Document ID — resourceLocator — required — the document.
- Artifact — options — required — one of `original`, `certificated`, `certificate-page`, `pades`, or `bundle` (default `certificated`). `pades` contains the signers' ICP-Brasil signatures plus the platform certification box and is available only when the document had digital-certificate signers.
- Put Output In Field — string — optional — output binary property name (default `data`).

**Example response:** Output is a binary item (no JSON payload other than metadata). The binary is the raw `application/pdf` file (or `application/zip` when Artifact is `bundle`), with a suggested filename of `{documentId}-{artifact}.pdf` (or `{documentId}-bundle.zip`). The mime type is taken from the response `Content-Type` header. A bundle contains `original`, `certificated`, and `certificate-page`, plus `pades` when present. Accompanying JSON: `{ "documentId", "fileName", "mimeType", "size" }`.

#### Download the document thumbnail

Downloads the document's thumbnail image into a binary property.

**Endpoint:** `GET /documents/{documentId}/thumbnail`

**Node parameters:**

- Document ID — resourceLocator — required — the document.
- Put Output In Field — string — optional — output binary property name (default `data`).

**Example response:** Output is a binary item. The binary is the raw `image/jpeg` thumbnail with suggested filename `{documentId}-thumbnail.jpg`. Accompanying JSON: `{ "documentId", "fileName", "mimeType", "size" }`.

#### Download a single page as a JPEG

Downloads a single rendered page of the document as a JPEG into a binary property.

**Endpoint:** `GET /documents/{documentId}/pages/{pageId}/download`

**Node parameters:**

- Document ID — resourceLocator — required — the document.
- Page ID — string — required — the page to download (from Get > `pages` array).
- Put Output In Field — string — optional — output binary property name (default `data`).

**Example response:** Output is a binary item. The binary is the raw `image/jpeg` page image with suggested filename `{documentId}-page-{pageId}.jpg`. Accompanying JSON: `{ "documentId", "fileName", "mimeType", "size" }`.

#### List the activity log for a document

Fetches every page of the document's activity log and returns one item shaped as `{documentId:string,activities:DocumentActivity[]}`. Pagination uses up to 100 entries per page, stops after the reported final page and rejects repeated pages or more than 10,000 pages.
