refresh_token=<current-refresh-token>
resource=https://api.assinafy.com.br
```

**Response:** the same flat token shape as Exchange Code, containing the new access token and replacement refresh token. Persist the replacement before another refresh. Serialize refreshes across all workers using the connection; replaying a used refresh token revokes the entire grant. Each refresh token is valid for 30 days and every refresh returns a new one with a fresh 30 days, so the connection expires only after 30 days without a refresh. Treat a timeout or ambiguous response as possibly successful: re-read the saved token before retrying, never retry blindly with the old one, and reconnect when the current token state cannot be established. Do not use this operation to refresh tokens belonging to an n8n-managed credential.

#### Revoke Token

**Endpoint:** `POST /oauth/revoke` — public transport; `application/x-www-form-urlencoded` body with client authentication.

**Node parameters:** Client ID, Client Secret (confidential clients), Token (required), and Token Type Hint (`access_token`, `refresh_token`, or Automatic/omitted).

**Complete form body, including optional fields (decoded):**

```text
client_id=<client-id>
client_secret=<client-secret>
token=<token-to-revoke>
token_type_hint=refresh_token
```

**HTTP response:** `200` with no required body. Unknown, malformed, expired, and already-revoked tokens also return success; this response does not prove the token existed. Failed client authentication returns `401`.

**n8n output:**

```json
{ "revoked": true }
```

#### Get User Info

**Endpoint:** `GET /oauth/userinfo`.

**Node parameters:** Access Token is optional. If supplied, it is sent as `Authorization: Bearer <access-token>` without the account credential. Otherwise, use the connected OAuth credential. There is no request body or query string. The token needs `openid`; `profile` allows `name`, and `email` allows the email claims.

**Complete response shape (flat HTTP response and n8n JSON output):**

```json
{
	"sub": "<authorized-user-id>",
	"name": "Example User",
	"email": "user@example.com",
	"email_verified": true
}
```

`sub` identifies the authorizing user. Other fields are optional/nullable according to the granted scopes. The node does not wrap these claims in `data`.

#### Get Metadata

**Endpoint:** `GET https://api.assinafy.com.br/.well-known/oauth-protected-resource`.

**Node parameters:** none. No authentication, request body, or query parameters. With an API-key credential targeting another environment, the same origin-level path is used on that environment.

**Complete response shape (flat HTTP response and n8n JSON output):**

```json
{
	"resource": "https://api.assinafy.com.br",
	"authorization_servers": ["https://auth.assinafy.com.br"],
	"scopes_supported": [
		"documents:read",
		"documents:write",
		"templates:read",
		"templates:write",
		"account:read",
		"openid",
		"profile",
		"email"
	],
	"bearer_methods_supported": ["header"]
}
```

The resource's scope list excludes `offline_access`; clients request refresh capability at the authorization server. Read the current metadata rather than assuming its scope list is fixed.

#### OAuth errors and retries

Token and revocation errors use a flat OAuth error object, surfaced as a node error:

```json
{
	"error": "invalid_grant",
	"error_description": "The authorization grant is invalid or expired."
}
```

| Status / error               | Meaning and next step                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `400 invalid_grant`          | Expired/reused code or refresh token, invalid PKCE verifier, callback mismatch, or revoked authorization. Reconnect when necessary.                                                                                                                                             |
| `400 invalid_target`         | Resource indicator is unsupported or differs from the authorized value.                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `400 unsupported_grant_type` | Use authorization code or refresh token.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `401 invalid_client`         | Client is unknown/disabled or client authentication failed. Check the client type and secret.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `401` on an API request      | Token is missing, expired, or invalid. Let the native credential renew or reconnect it.                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `403` on an API request      | With `WWW-Authenticate: Bearer error="insufficient_scope", scope="…"`, add that scope to the credential and reconnect; do not retry. Otherwise the workspace, the user's role, or the endpoint is outside the grant; additional scopes cannot enable owner-only administration. |

Exchange Code, Refresh Token, and Revoke Token never automatically retry, including after HTTP 429. Metadata and UserInfo use the shared bounded rate-limit retry behavior. All five preserve the selected environment and use the common request timeout.

---

### Authentication

Login, Social Login, Complete Two-Factor Login, Request Password Reset, and Reset Password are public and do not send the configured API key. Link Social Login and the API-key/password management routes accept the configured API key or an Access Token when the node exposes that field. A Bearer-only call can run without an action credential, in which case it uses the production base URL; select a Sandbox credential to target sandbox.

#### Login

Exchanges an email and password for a JWT access token, returning the user profile and accessible accounts.

**Endpoint:** `POST /login`

**Node parameters:**

- Email (`authEmail`) — string — required — user's login email.
- Password (`authPassword`) — string (password) — required — user's password.

**Example request:**

```json
{
	"email": "name@example.com",
	"password": "<password>"
}
```

**Example response:**

```json
{
	"access_token": "<access-token>",
	"user": {
		"id": "bgjazeo5r9v2lq7l36dx48np",
		"name": "Example User",
		"email": "john.smith@example.com",
		"telephone": "<telephone>",
		"government_id": "<government-id>",
		"is_email_verified": false,
		"has_accepted_terms": true,
		"created_at": "2023-03-03T11:51:34Z",
		"to_be_deleted_at": null
	},
	"accounts": [
		{
			"id": "<user-id-1>",
			"name": "JS",
			"roles": ["owner"],
			"is_delete_allowed": true,
			"created_at": "2023-03-03T11:51:34Z"
		}
	]
}
```

For a user with two-factor authentication enabled, the response carries an `mfa_token` to pass to **Complete Two-Factor Login** instead of an access token.

#### Social Login

Trades a social provider access/ID token (currently Google only) for an Assinafy access token.

**Endpoint:** `POST /authentication/social-login`

**Node parameters:**

- Provider (`provider`) — options — optional — social provider; only `google` is available (defaults to `google`).
- Social Token (`socialToken`) — string (password) — required — access or ID token received from the social provider.
- Has Accepted Terms (`hasAcceptedTerms`) — boolean — optional — indicates the user accepted the terms (defaults to `true`).

**Example request:**

```json
{
	"provider": "google",
	"token": "<social-provider-token>",
	"has_accepted_terms": true
}
```

**Example response:**

```json
{
	"access_token": "<access-token>",
	"user": {
		"id": "bgjazeo5r9v2lq7l36dx48np",
		"name": "Example User",
		"email": "john.smith@example.com",
		"telephone": "<telephone>",
		"government_id": "<government-id>",
		"is_email_verified": false,
		"has_accepted_terms": true,
		"created_at": "2023-03-03T11:51:34Z",
		"to_be_deleted_at": null
	},
	"accounts": [
		{
			"id": "<user-id-1>",
			"name": "JS",
			"roles": ["owner"],
			"is_delete_allowed": true,
			"created_at": "2023-03-03T11:51:34Z"
		}
	]
}
```

#### Link Social Login

Links a social identity provider to an already authenticated Assinafy user. Google is the currently exposed provider. Authentication may use the configured `X-Api-Key`; when **Access Token** is provided, the node uses `Authorization: Bearer <access-token>` for this call instead.

**Endpoint:** `POST /auth/link-social-login`

**Node parameters:**

- Provider — options — optional — currently `google` (default).
- Social Token — string (password) — required — access or ID token received from the provider.
- Access Token — string (password) — optional — Assinafy JWT; leave empty to use the configured API key.

**Example request:**

```json
{
	"provider": "google",
	"token": "<social-provider-token>"
}
```

**Example response:**

```json
{}
```

The node returns the unwrapped `data` value unchanged. An invalid or expired provider token returns an authentication error.

#### Create API Key

Generates a new API key for the user. Authentication can use a Bearer access token from Login/Social Login or the configured API key. Generating a new key deletes the previous one. The returned key is shown in full only here — store it immediately and never use it from a frontend application.

**Endpoint:** `POST /users/api-keys`

**Node parameters:**

- Access Token (`accessToken`) — string (password) — optional — JWT from Login or Social Login. Leave empty to use the configured API key.
- Password (`authPassword`) — string (password) — required — user's password.

**Example request:**

```json
{
	"password": "<password>"
}
```

**Example response:**

```json
{
	"api_key": "<api-key>"
}
```

#### Get API Key (Masked)

Retrieves a masked view of the current API key. For security the full key is never returned; if no key has been generated yet, `null` is returned.

**Endpoint:** `GET /users/api-keys`

**Node parameters:**

- Access Token (`accessToken`) — string (password) — optional — JWT from Login or Social Login. Leave empty to use the configured API key.

**Example response:**

```json
{
	"api_key": "<masked-api-key>"
}
```

#### Delete API Key

Revokes the current API key using an optional Bearer access token or the configured API key. This can invalidate the credential running other workflows. The node returns a synthesized confirmation object for the API's empty response.

**Endpoint:** `DELETE /users/api-keys`

**Request body:** none.

**Node parameters:**

- Access Token (`accessToken`) — string (password) — optional — JWT from Login or Social Login. Leave empty to use the configured API key.

**Example response:**

```json
{
	"deleted": true
}
```

#### Change Password

Changes the authenticated user's password using an optional Bearer access token or the configured API key, plus the current and new passwords.

**Endpoint:** `PUT /authentication/change-password`

**Node parameters:**

- Email (`authEmail`) — string — required — user's email.
- Access Token (`accessToken`) — string (password) — optional — JWT from Login or Social Login. Leave empty to use the configured API key.
- Current Password (`currentPassword`) — string (password) — required — the current password.
- New Password (`newPassword`) — string (password) — required — the new password to set.

**Example request:**

```json
{
	"email": "john.smith@example.com",
	"password": "<current-password>",
	"new_password": "<new-password>"
}
```

**Example response:**

```json
{
	"email": "john.smith@example.com"
}
```

#### Request Password Reset

Emails password-reset instructions to a user. Typically used when the user forgot their password or has not set one yet. No authentication required.

**Endpoint:** `PUT /authentication/request-password-reset`

**Node parameters:**

- Email (`authEmail`) — string — required — user's email.

**Example request:**

```json
{
	"email": "john.smith@example.com"
}
```

**Example response:**

```json
{
	"email": "john.smith@example.com"
}
```

#### Reset Password

Sets a new password using the reset token received by email. No authentication required.

**Endpoint:** `PUT /authentication/reset-password`

**Node parameters:**

- Email (`authEmail`) — string — required — user's email.
- Reset Token (`resetToken`) — string (password) — optional — token received in the password-reset email; included in the body as `token` only when provided.
- New Password (`newPassword`) — string (password) — required — the new password to set.

**Example request:**

```json
{
	"email": "john.smith@example.com",
	"token": "<reset-token>",
	"new_password": "<new-password>"
}
```

**Example response:**

```json
{
	"email": "john.smith@example.com"
}
```

#### Two-factor authentication

When two-factor authentication is enabled for a user, **Login** returns an `mfa_token` instead of a session; **Complete Two-Factor Login** exchanges it for the access token. The user-scoped operations below use the optional **Access Token** (a JWT from Login or Complete Two-Factor Login) as `Authorization: Bearer`, or the configured credential when it is empty. Codes and secrets in their outputs are credentials; restrict execution retention and access.

##### Complete Two-Factor Login

**Endpoint:** `POST /authentication/mfa/verify` — public transport (no account authentication).

**Node parameters:**

- MFA Token (`mfaToken`) — string (password) — required — the `mfa_token` from Login; single-use, expires 5 minutes after login.
- Code (`mfaCode`) — string (password) — required — a 6-digit authenticator code or a recovery code such as `ABCD-EFGH-JKMN`.

**Example request:**

```json
{ "mfa_token": "<mfa-token>", "code": "123456" }
```

**Example response:** the same session shape as **Login** (`access_token`, `user`, `accounts`). Errors: `400` invalid or already-used code; `401` challenge expired, already used, or too many attempts.

##### List Two-Factor Methods

**Endpoint:** `GET /users/self/mfa`

**Node parameters:** Access Token — optional.

**Example response:**

```json
{
	"methods": [
		{
			"id": "<method-id>",
			"type": "totp",
			"label": "Phone",
			"confirmed_at": "2026-10-01T12:00:00Z",
			"last_used_at": "2026-10-05T09:30:00Z"
		}
	],
	"recovery_codes_remaining": 10
}
```

##### Start Authenticator Enrollment

Creates an unconfirmed authenticator method. The shared secret is returned only by this call. Two-factor authentication is not active until the enrollment is confirmed.

**Endpoint:** `POST /users/self/mfa/totp`

**Node parameters:** Access Token — optional; Label (`mfaLabel`) — string — optional.

**Example request:**

```json
{ "label": "Phone" }
```

**Example response:**

```json
{
	"id": "<method-id>",
	"secret": "<base32-secret>",
	"provisioning_uri": "otpauth://totp/Assinafy:user%40example.com?secret=<base32-secret>&issuer=Assinafy"
}
```

##### Confirm Authenticator Enrollment

Activates the method with a live code from the new device and returns the recovery codes, shown only once. From then on every login requires a second factor. Confirming while a method of the same type is already confirmed replaces it and reissues recovery codes, and then requires re-authentication by password or by a code from the current device or a recovery code.

**Endpoint:** `PUT /users/self/mfa/totp/confirm`

**Node parameters:**

- Access Token — optional.
- Method ID (`mfaMethodId`) — string — required — the `id` from Start Authenticator Enrollment.
- Code (`mfaCode`) — string (password) — required — live code from the new device.
- Re-Authentication Password (`reauthPassword`) — optional — sent as `password`.
- Re-Authentication Code (`reauthCode`) — optional — sent as `reauth_code`.

**Example request:**

```json
{ "id": "<method-id>", "code": "123456", "reauth_code": "654321" }
```

**Example response:**

```json
{ "recovery_codes": ["ABCD-EFGH-JKMN", "PQRS-TUVW-XYZ2"] }
```

Errors: `400` invalid enrollment code or missing/invalid re-authentication; `404` unknown method.

##### Regenerate Recovery Codes

Issues ten new recovery codes and invalidates the previous set.

**Endpoint:** `POST /users/self/mfa/recovery-codes`

**Node parameters:** Access Token — optional; Re-Authentication Password (`password`) or Re-Authentication Code (`code`, a live authenticator code or an existing recovery code, which is consumed) — at least one required.

**Example request:**

```json
{ "password": "<password>" }
```

**Example response:**

```json
{ "recovery_codes": ["ABCD-EFGH-JKMN", "PQRS-TUVW-XYZ2"] }
```

##### Remove Two-Factor Method

Removes an enrolled method. Removing the last method also discards the recovery codes.

**Endpoint:** `DELETE /users/self/mfa/{methodId}` — JSON body.

**Node parameters:** Access Token — optional; Method ID — required; Re-Authentication Password (`password`) or Re-Authentication Code (`code`) — at least one required.

**Example request:**

```json
{ "code": "123456" }
```

**Example response:**

```json
{ "is_mfa_enabled": false }
```

Errors: `400` missing or invalid re-authentication; `404` unknown method.

---

### Workspace

A workspace maps to an Assinafy "account". These operations manage the workspaces (accounts) accessible to your API key. Note that account-scoped resources elsewhere in the node use the **Account ID** from the credential; the Workspace resource instead targets accounts directly via the top-level `/accounts` collection and a per-workspace ID.

#### Create a workspace

Creates a new workspace (account).

**Endpoint:** `POST /accounts`

**Node parameters:**

- Name — string — required — Workspace name.
- Additional Fields — collection — optional — extra workspace attributes:
  - Notification Sender — options — optional — `User` (send as the acting user) or `Account` (show the workspace), sent as `notification_sender_type`.
  - Primary Color — color — optional — sent as `primary_color` only when set.
  - Secondary Color — color — optional — sent as `secondary_color` only when set.

**Example request:**

```json
{
	"name": "sdk-example-workspace",
	"notification_sender_type": "Account",
	"primary_color": "1a73e8",
	"secondary_color": "ff8800"
}
```

**Example response:**

```json
{
	"id": "<workspace-id>",
	"name": "sdk-example-workspace",
	"primary_color": "1a73e8",
	"secondary_color": "ff8800",
	"created_at": "2026-07-20T19:00:30Z"
}
```

#### Delete a workspace

Deletes a workspace by ID.

**Endpoint:** `DELETE /accounts/{workspaceId}`

**Node parameters:**

- Workspace ID — string — required — ID of the workspace to delete.
- Force — boolean — optional (default `false`) — when true, sends `{ "force": true }` to cancel an active paid subscription and delete immediately. Use only with a confirmed disposable workspace.

**Example request body when Force is enabled:**

```json
{ "force": true }
```

**Example response:**

The API returns an empty array (`data: []`); the SDK returns a synthesized confirmation object instead:

```json
{
	"deleted": true,
	"workspaceId": "<workspace-id>"
}
```

#### Get a workspace

Retrieves a single workspace by ID.

**Endpoint:** `GET /accounts/{workspaceId}`

**Node parameters:**

- Workspace ID — string — required — ID of the workspace to retrieve.

**Example response:**

```json
{
	"id": "<workspace-id>",
	"name": "Example Workspace",
	"primary_color": null,
	"secondary_color": null,
	"created_at": "2026-05-12T18:05:11Z"
}
```

#### List accessible workspaces

Lists the workspaces (accounts) accessible to the API key. Supports pagination via the standard `Return All` / `Limit` controls; the SDK reads the `X-Pagination-*` response headers.

**Endpoint:** `GET /accounts`

**Node parameters:**

- Return All — boolean — optional — when true, fetches every page; default false.
- Limit — number — optional — max results to return when Return All is false.

**Example response:**

```json
[
	{
		"id": "<workspace-id>",
		"name": "Example Workspace",
		"primary_color": null,
		"secondary_color": null,
		"created_at": "2026-05-12T18:05:11Z"
	}
]
```

#### Update a workspace

Updates an existing workspace. At least one update field is required — the node throws `At least one update field is required` if none are supplied.

**Endpoint:** `PUT /accounts/{workspaceId}`

**Node parameters:**

- Workspace ID — string — required — ID of the workspace to update.
- Update Fields — collection — required (at least one) — fields to change:
  - Name — string — optional — sent as `name`.
  - Notification Sender — options — optional — `User` or `Account`, sent as `notification_sender_type`.
  - Primary Color — color — optional — sent as `primary_color`.
  - Secondary Color — color — optional — sent as `secondary_color`.

**Example request:**

```json
{
	"name": "Renamed Example Workspace",
	"notification_sender_type": "User",
	"primary_color": "1a73e8"
}
```

**Example response:**

```json
{
	"id": "<workspace-id>",
	"name": "Renamed Example Workspace",
	"primary_color": "1a73e8",
	"secondary_color": "ff8800",
	"created_at": "2026-05-12T18:05:11Z"
}
```

#### Get Account Statistics

Returns document-funnel statistics for one workspace. The node emits one n8n item per statistics row.

**Endpoint:** `GET /accounts/{workspaceId}/stats`

**Node parameters:**

- Workspace ID — string — required — workspace to report.
- Granularity — options — optional — `monthly` (default, last twelve months) or `daily` (days in one month).
- Month — string — required only for daily granularity — strict `YYYY-MM` format.

**Example request (query):**

```json
{
	"granularity": "daily",
	"month": "2026-08"
}
```

**Example response:**

```json
[
	{
		"period": "2026-08-01",
		"documents_uploaded": 2,
		"documents_sent": 2,
		"signature_requests": 3,
		"signature_requests_notification_email": 2,
		"signature_requests_notification_whatsapp": 1,
		"signature_requests_notification_bypass": 0,
		"signature_requests_verification_email": 2,
		"signature_requests_verification_whatsapp": 1,
		"signature_requests_verification_bypass": 0,
		"signature_requests_verification_digital_certificate": 0,
		"signature_requests_viewed": 2,
		"signature_requests_completed": 1,
		"documents_certified": 1
	}
]
```

Monthly rows cover the last 12 months and daily rows cover every day of the requested month; both are zero-filled and ordered most recent first. Notification counters split requests by delivery channel, so a request sent through more than one channel counts once in each channel and those counters can total more than `signature_requests`. Verification counters split requests by their single verification method and therefore total exactly `signature_requests`.

#### Get User Statistics

Returns aggregate document-funnel statistics across workspaces accessible to the current user. The node emits one n8n item per row.

**Endpoint:** `GET /users/self/stats`

**Node parameters:**

- Granularity — options — optional — `monthly` (default) or `daily`.
- Month — string — required only for daily granularity — strict `YYYY-MM` format.

**Example request (query):**

```json
{ "granularity": "monthly" }
```

**Example response:**

```json
[
	{
		"period": "2026-08",
		"documents_uploaded": 20,
		"documents_sent": 18,
		"signature_requests": 24,
		"signature_requests_notification_email": 20,
		"signature_requests_notification_whatsapp": 4,
		"signature_requests_notification_bypass": 0,
		"signature_requests_verification_email": 18,
		"signature_requests_verification_whatsapp": 2,
		"signature_requests_verification_bypass": 0,
		"signature_requests_verification_digital_certificate": 4,
		"signature_requests_viewed": 17,
		"signature_requests_completed": 15,
		"documents_certified": 12
	}
]
```

As with account statistics, the series is zero-filled and ordered most recent first. Notification counters can overlap when a request uses multiple delivery channels; verification counters form a non-overlapping breakdown by verification method.

#### Get Notification Preferences

Returns the authenticated user's owner-facing document and contract lifecycle email preferences. Account and security messages such as password resets, workspace invitations, and account deletion are not configurable through this route.

**Endpoint:** `GET /users/self/notification-preferences`

**Node parameters:** none.

**Example response:**

```json
{
	"DocumentCompleted": true,
	"SignerDeclined": true,
	"DocumentCancelled": true,
	"DocumentAboutToExpire": true,
	"DocumentExpired": true,
	"DocumentExpirationReset": true,
	"DocumentProcessingFailed": true,
	"TemplateProcessingFailed": true,
	"SignerWhatsappFailed": true,
	"ContractRenewalNotice": true,
	"ContractExpiring": true,
	"ContractExpired": true,
	"ContractRenewed": true
}
```

All thirteen keys are always returned; `true` means that email is enabled.

#### Update Notification Preferences

Merges one or more owner-facing email preferences into the authenticated user's current map. Omitted keys keep their existing values, and the API returns all thirteen preferences. Setting a key to `false` disables that email for this user across every account they belong to. The node rejects an empty collection or a non-boolean expression value before sending the request.

**Endpoint:** `PUT /users/self/notification-preferences`

**Node parameters:**

- Preferences — collection — required (at least one) — any partial selection of:
  - Contract Expired (`ContractExpired`) — boolean — contract ended without renewal.
  - Contract Expiring (`ContractExpiring`) — boolean — contract ends in 45 or 30 days.
  - Contract Renewal Notice (`ContractRenewalNotice`) — boolean — notice period starts.
  - Contract Renewed (`ContractRenewed`) — boolean — contract automatically enters its next term.
  - Document About to Expire (`DocumentAboutToExpire`) — boolean.
  - Document Cancelled (`DocumentCancelled`) — boolean.
  - Document Completed (`DocumentCompleted`) — boolean.
  - Document Expiration Reset (`DocumentExpirationReset`) — boolean.
  - Document Expired (`DocumentExpired`) — boolean.
  - Document Processing Failed (`DocumentProcessingFailed`) — boolean.
  - Signer Declined (`SignerDeclined`) — boolean.
  - Signer WhatsApp Failed (`SignerWhatsappFailed`) — boolean.
  - Template Processing Failed (`TemplateProcessingFailed`) — boolean.

**Example request:**

```json
{
	"DocumentCompleted": false,
	"SignerDeclined": true
}
```

**Example response:**

```json
{
	"DocumentCompleted": false,
	"SignerDeclined": true,
	"DocumentCancelled": true,
	"DocumentAboutToExpire": true,
	"DocumentExpired": true,
	"DocumentExpirationReset": true,
	"DocumentProcessingFailed": true,
	"TemplateProcessingFailed": true,
	"SignerWhatsappFailed": true,
	"ContractRenewalNotice": true,
	"ContractExpiring": true,
	"ContractExpired": true,
	"ContractRenewed": true
}
```

#### Get Current User

Returns the authenticated user (the owner of the API key) as the direct `AuthUser` object shown below. This operation is not account-scoped.

**Endpoint:** `GET /users/self`

**Node parameters:** none.

**Example response:**

```json
{
	"id": "<user-id>",
	"name": "Example User",
	"email": "user@example.com",
	"telephone": null,
	"government_id": null,
	"is_email_verified": true,
	"has_accepted_terms": true,
	"created_at": "2026-05-12T18:05:11Z",
	"to_be_deleted_at": null
}
```

The node unwraps the standard `{ status, message, data }` envelope and otherwise leaves `data` unchanged.

#### Get Theme

Returns the workspace branding (display name, hex colors, and logo URL if any).

**Endpoint:** `GET /accounts/{workspaceId}/theme`

**Node parameters:**

- Workspace ID — string — required — the workspace whose theme to fetch.

**Example response:**

```json
{
	"account_name": "Example Workspace",
	"primary_color": "2072b9",
	"secondary_color": "ffffff",
	"logo": null
}
```

#### Upload Logo

Uploads (or replaces) the workspace logo from an incoming binary item. Sent as `multipart/form-data` with a single `file` part (PNG or JPEG).

**Endpoint:** `POST /accounts/{workspaceId}/logo`

**Node parameters:**

- Workspace ID — string — required — the workspace to set the logo on.
- Binary Property — string — required — name of the binary property holding the logo image (PNG or JPEG; default `data`). A missing or generic `application/octet-stream` MIME type is accepted only when PNG/JPEG magic bytes identify an allowed image.

**Example request:**

```text
multipart/form-data:
  file: <binary PNG or JPEG>
```

**Example response:**

```json
{ "mime_type": "image/png", "version": 1784574086, "updated_at": "2026-07-20T19:01:26Z" }
```

#### Download Logo

Downloads the current workspace logo as a binary image on the output item. Returns HTTP 404 if no logo is set.

**Endpoint:** `GET /accounts/{workspaceId}/logo`

**Node parameters:**

- Workspace ID — string — required — the workspace whose logo to download.
- Put Output In Field — string — the binary property to write the image into (default `data`).

**Example output item:**

```json
{
	"json": {
		"workspaceId": "<workspace-id>",
		"fileName": "<workspace-id>-logo.png",
		"mimeType": "image/png",
		"size": 69
	},
	"binary": {
		"data": { "fileName": "<workspace-id>-logo.png", "mimeType": "image/png", "data": "<base64>" }
	}
}
```

#### Delete Logo

Removes the workspace logo.

**Endpoint:** `DELETE /accounts/{workspaceId}/logo`

**Request body:** none.

**Node parameters:**

- Workspace ID — string — required — the workspace to clear the logo on.

**Example response:** the API returns an empty array (`data: []`); the SDK returns a synthesized confirmation:

```json
{ "deleted": true, "workspaceId": "<workspace-id>" }
```

## Additional operation contracts

Each request below is relative to the production API origin. Path IDs come from the node fields described per resource. JSON bodies use `application/json`; absent bodies are omitted. Parameters show all optional fields; response schemas include the HTTP envelope before node unwrapping. Referenced schemas resolve to the shared catalog above. Errors are surfaced as n8n errors; structured `errors` entries identify invalid fields, and 401/403/404/409 distinguish authentication, permissions/plan, missing records and conflicts.

### Envelope

Use **Envelope → Estimate Cost**, then **Create** after every PDF reaches `metadata_ready`. **Envelope (JSON)** (`envelopeBody`) supplies the full request object for Create, Estimate Cost, Update and Add Document. **Envelope ID** selects an existing envelope; Remove Document also requires Document ID and Resend Notification requires Signer ID. Create alone exposes **Idempotency Key**, sent unchanged as `Idempotency-Key`. List supports Search/Status; List and Get Activities support Return All/Limit; activities use Sort (`id`/`-id`). Download uses Artifact (default `certificated`) and Put Output In Field (default `data`). Lists emit one item per result; downloads use n8n binary storage with `{envelopeId,fileName,mimeType,size}` metadata; other outputs unwrap `data`.

Store the returned envelope/document IDs before proceeding. One invitation covers a signer's documents; HTTP 200 confirms creation, not delivery. Check each signer's notification history. `max_total_credits: 0` permits only a zero-credit transaction. Idempotency keys are permanent within the workspace; reuse the same key only for the same intended envelope, even after a lost response. The API enforces plan features, membership, field placement, methods, budgets and lifecycle rules. Existing document events include `envelope_id`; download only after the desired per-document certified artifacts exist. Cancel and Remove Document can notify signers. Delete requires closed documents. OAuth uses `documents:read` for reads and `documents:write` for mutations; API keys also work.

#### List envelopes

**Endpoint:** `GET /accounts/{accountId}/envelopes`

The workspace's envelopes, the most recent first: each with its documents in order, their statuses and signers, and the counts by status.

**HTTP parameters:**

```json
[
  {
    "name": "accountId",
    "in": "path",
    "description": "Workspace account ID.",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  {
    "name": "page",
    "in": "query",
    "description": "Page number.",
    "schema": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1
    }
  },
  {
    "name": "per-page",
    "in": "query",
    "description": "Records per page: 20 by default, at most 50; a larger value answers 50 a page.",
    "schema": {
      "type": "integer",
      "default": 20,
      "maximum": 50,
      "minimum": 1
    }
  },
  {
    "name": "search",
    "in": "query",
    "description": "Part of the envelope's name.",
    "required": false,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  {
    "name": "status",
    "in": "query",
    "description": "`open`: envelopes with a document still open; `closed`: every document signed, declined, cancelled, expired or failed.",
    "required": false,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "open",
        "closed"
      ]
    }
  }
]
```

#### Create envelope

**Endpoint:** `POST /accounts/{accountId}/envelopes`

Send several documents to the same signers at once. Each document gets its own assignment and keeps its own status, certificate and downloads; one invitation goes to each signer, through the methods sent for them, for all the documents they are on, from the first of them. A send that fails is recorded on that signer's request (`notification_history`) and doesn't undo the envelope: `200` means the envelope was created, not that every invitation was delivered. The envelope is charged as one document, whatever its documents, each signer's notifications and identity check once for the whole envelope, and each digital-certificate signature on every document it is made on. Every document must be uploaded and processed (`metadata_ready`) with no assignment, and every listed signer must be on at least one document. Each document is signed on its virtual page or, with `collect`, on the fields placed on its pages, as on an assignment; a digital-certificate signer signs only virtual documents, alone on each. Needs the Envelopes feature on the workspace's plan (`403` with code `PlanRestricted` otherwise). A refused request creates and charges nothing; `errors` names every field at fault.

**HTTP parameters:**

```json
[
  {
    "name": "accountId",
    "in": "path",
    "description": "Workspace account ID.",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  {
    "name": "Idempotency-Key",
    "in": "header",
    "description": "Any text, case-sensitive and new for each envelope you mean to create (a UUID, say). Retrying with the same key after a lost response answers the envelope the first request created, and creates and charges nothing, whatever the body says. A key belongs to its workspace and doesn't expire. `409` with code `IdempotencyKeyInUse` if the first request is still running: try again. An empty value counts as no key, as on template documents.",
    "required": false,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  }
]
```

**Full request body contract:**

```json
{
  "required": true,
  "content": {
    "application/json": {
      "schema": {
        "$ref": "#/components/schemas/EnvelopeCreateRequest"
      }
    }
  }
}
```

**HTTP 200:** The created envelope

```json
{
  "application/json": {
    "schema": {
      "type": "object",
      "allOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Envelope"
        },
        {
          "properties": {
            "data": {
              "$ref": "#/components/schemas/DocumentEnvelope"
            }
          },
          "type": "object"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

**HTTP 400:** Refused as sent; nothing was created or charged. Each field at fault is an entry of `errors` (a list refused as a whole is not read item by item), an item of a list named by its index (`documents[1].signer_ids`): `name`; `message` (not text); `signers` and `documents` (missing, empty, not a list, or more than 20 signers or 20 documents; `documents` also when its documents hold more than 2,000 fields between them, a virtual document's being one per signer, or more than 100 copy receivers); `signers[i].id` (missing, listed twice, not a signer of the workspace, or on no document); `signers[i].verification_method` (not a code); `signers[i].notification_methods` (not a list of codes); `signers[i].step` (sent at all); `documents[i].id` (missing, listed twice, or a document that can't take the assignment: not found, not `metadata_ready`, or already assigned); `documents[i].method` (other than `virtual` or `collect`); `documents[i].entries` (missing for `collect`, or not a list of pages with fields naming their `signer_id`); `documents[i].expires_at` (not text, a number or null); `documents[i].signer_ids` (missing, not a list of ids, a signer not listed once in `signers`, for `collect` not exactly the signers on its fields, or a digital-certificate signer on a `collect` document or with another signer). A page or field the assignment doesn't find on a `collect` document, a method code the platform doesn't know, a verification and notification that don't pair, a signer without the e-mail or WhatsApp number their method needs, an `embedded` other than `true`, `false`, `1` or `0` (`null` reads as left out, as on an assignment) or set for a signer verified other than by Bypass, Email or WhatsApp, a balance too low for the envelope, an `expires_at` or `copy_receiver_ids` the assignment refuses, and a document another request assigned or put back into processing meanwhile answer `400` with `message` only, as on an assignment.

```json
{
  "application/json": {
    "schema": {
      "allOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ErrorEnvelope"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

**HTTP 403:** The workspace's plan lacks the Envelopes feature, or a signer's method or embedded signing the plan lacks (`PlanRestricted`); or an OAuth app's token lacks `documents:write` (`InsufficientScope`). Also a member deactivated in the workspace, a workspace scheduled for deletion, or an OAuth token of another workspace.

```json
{
  "application/json": {
    "schema": {
      "type": "object",
      "allOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ErrorEnvelope"
        },
        {
          "properties": {
            "status": {
              "type": "integer"
            },
            "code": {
              "type": "string",
              "enum": [
                "PlanRestricted",
                "InsufficientScope"
              ]
            }
          },
          "type": "object"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

**HTTP 404:** The requested resource does not exist.

```json
{
  "application/json": {
    "schema": {
      "type": "object",
      "allOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ErrorEnvelope"
        },
        {
          "properties": {
            "status": {
              "type": "integer"
            },
            "message": {
              "type": "string"
            }
          },
          "type": "object"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

**HTTP 409:** The envelope costs more than `max_total_credits` (`CreditCeilingExceeded`), or another request with this `Idempotency-Key` is still running (`IdempotencyKeyInUse`).

```json
{
  "application/json": {
    "schema": {
      "type": "object",
      "allOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ErrorEnvelope"
        },
        {
          "properties": {
            "status": {
              "type": "integer"
            },
            "code": {
              "type": "string",
              "enum": [
                "CreditCeilingExceeded",
                "IdempotencyKeyInUse"
              ]
            },
            "total_credits": {
              "description": "With `CreditCeilingExceeded`: what the envelope costs now.",
              "type": "number"
            },
            "max_total_credits": {
              "description": "With `CreditCeilingExceeded`: the ceiling it was sent with.",
              "type": "number"
            }
          },
          "type": "object"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

**HTTP 401:** Missing or invalid credentials.

```json
{
  "application/json": {
    "schema": {
      "type": "object",
      "allOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ErrorEnvelope"
        },
        {
          "properties": {
            "status": {
              "type": "integer"
            },
            "message": {
              "type": "string"
            }
          },
          "type": "object"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

**HTTP 500:** Unexpected server error.

```json
{
  "application/json": {
    "schema": {
      "type": "object",
      "allOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ErrorEnvelope"
        },
        {
          "properties": {
            "status": {
              "type": "integer"
            },
            "message": {
              "type": "string"

#### Estimate envelope cost

**Endpoint:** `POST /accounts/{accountId}/envelopes/estimate-cost`

What creating the envelope would cost, creating and charging nothing. Takes the creation body and checks it as creation does (`max_total_credits` aside); answers as an assignment's estimate: the envelope counts as one document (`documents` is 1, an extra one when the plan's allowance doesn't cover it), each signer's notifications and identity check count once for the whole envelope, and each digital-certificate signature on every document it is made on, as creation charges them.

**HTTP parameters:**

```json
[
  {
    "name": "accountId",
    "in": "path",
    "description": "Workspace account ID.",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  }
]
```

}

What creation checks while it creates each document (a signer's missing contact, a page or field not found, the balance) is not checked here: `has_sufficient_resources` says what the balance allows.

#### Delete envelope

**Endpoint:** `DELETE /accounts/{accountId}/envelopes/{envelopeId}`

Delete an envelope once every document of it is closed: certificated, declined, cancelled, expired or failed. Before that it answers `409` with code `EnvelopeOpen`: cancel it first. The envelope is gone: it is no longer listed and reads `404`, and a create retried with its `Idempotency-Key` no longer answers it. Its documents stay as ordinary documents, with their certificates, downloads and webhooks; `GET /v1/documents/{documentId}?expand=envelope_id` reads null for them.

**HTTP parameters:**

```json
[
  {
    "name": "accountId",
    "in": "path",
    "description": "Workspace account ID.",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  {
    "name": "envelopeId",
    "in": "path",
    "description": "The envelope ID.",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  }
]
```

**Request body:** none.

**HTTP 200:** Envelope deleted

```json
{
  "application/json": {
    "schema": {
      "type": "object",
      "allOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Envelope"
        },
        {
          "properties": {
            "data": {
              "type": "array",
              "items": []
            }
          },
          "type": "object"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

**HTTP 403:** An OAuth app's token lacks `documents:write` (`InsufficientScope`). Also a member deactivated in the workspace, a workspace scheduled for deletion, or an OAuth token of another workspace.

```json
{
  "application/json": {
    "schema": {

      "allOf": [
        {

        },
        {
          "properties": {
            "status": {
              "type": "integer"
            },
            "code": {
              "type": "string",
              "enum": [
                "InsufficientScope"
              ]
            }
          },
          "type": "object"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

**HTTP 404:** The requested resource does not exist.

```json
{
  "application/json": {
    "schema": {
      "type": "object",
      "allOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ErrorEnvelope"

#### Update envelope

**Endpoint:** `PATCH /accounts/{accountId}/envelopes/{envelopeId}`

Rename the envelope, change its message or put its documents in a new order; send only what changes. Nothing is sent to anyone: the reads and every notice sent from then on (a reminder, a cancel, a document added) use the new name, message and order. The message is also each document's assignment `message`. Works on a closed envelope too. A refused request changes nothing; `errors` names every field at fault.

**HTTP parameters:**

```json
[
  {
    "name": "accountId",
    "in": "path",
    "description": "Workspace account ID.",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  {
    "name": "envelopeId",
    "in": "path",
    "description": "The envelope ID.",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  }
]
```

**Full request body contract:**

```json
{
  "required": true,
  "content": {
    "application/json": {
      "schema": {
        "properties": {
          "name": {
            "description": "What the invitation calls what is being signed. `<` is refused (`400`).",
            "type": "string",
            "maxLength": 255,
            "minLength": 1
          },
          "message": {
            "description": "The message sent to every signer from now on; null for none.",
            "type": [
              "string",
              "null"
            ]
          },
          "document_ids": {
            "description": "Every document of the envelope, each once, in the new order; `position` follows it. An envelope listing no document takes no order.",
            "type": "array",
            "items": {
              "type": "string"
            },
            "minItems": 1
          }
        },
        "type": "object"
      }
    }
  }
}
```

**HTTP 200:** The envelope as changed

```json
{
  "application/json": {
    "schema": {
      "type": "object",
      "allOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Envelope"
        },
        {
          "properties": {
            "data": {
              "$ref": "#/components/schemas/DocumentEnvelope"
            }
          },
          "type": "object"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

**HTTP 400:** Refused as sent; nothing changed. Each field at fault is an entry of `errors`: `name` (empty, not text, over 255 characters, or holding `<`), `message` (not text), `document_ids` (empty, or not a list of every document of the envelope, each once).

```json
{
  "application/json": {
    "schema": {
      "allOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ErrorEnvelope"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

#### List envelope activities

**Endpoint:** `GET /accounts/{accountId}/envelopes/{envelopeId}/activities`

The envelope's trail: the activities of every document it has had, one taken out included (not one deleted, whose own list answers 404), newest first (`sort=id` for oldest first), each naming its document (`document_id`, `document_name`). Each entry is otherwise a document activity, as `GET /v1/documents/{documentId}/activities` answers it. Paged: 20 per page by default; the `X-Pagination-Total-Count`, `X-Pagination-Page-Count`, `X-Pagination-Current-Page` and `X-Pagination-Per-Page` headers describe the full list, and a page past the end returns an empty `data`.

**HTTP parameters:**

```json
[
  {
    "name": "accountId",
    "in": "path",
    "description": "Workspace account ID.",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  {
    "name": "envelopeId",
    "in": "path",
    "description": "The envelope ID.",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  {
    "name": "sort",
    "in": "query",
    "description": "`-id` (default, newest first) or `id`.",
    "schema": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  {
    "name": "page",
    "in": "query",
    "description": "Page number.",
    "schema": {
      "type": "integer",
      "minimum": 1
    }
  },
  {
    "name": "per-page",
    "in": "query",
    "description": "Records per page: 20 by default, at most 50; a larger value answers 50 a page.",
    "schema": {
      "type": "integer",
      "default": 20,
      "maximum": 50,
      "minimum": 1
    }
  }
]
```

**Request body:** none.

**HTTP 200:** The envelope's activities

```json
{
  "application/json": {
    "schema": {
      "type": "object",
      "allOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Envelope"

            "data": {
              "type": "array",
              "items": {
                "$ref": "#/components/schemas/EnvelopeActivity"
              }
            }
          },
          "type": "object"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

#### Download envelope artifacts

**Endpoint:** `GET /accounts/{accountId}/envelopes/{envelopeId}/download/{artifactName}`

One zip, named after the envelope, of the chosen artifact of every document of it, in order. Each file is named after its document's position (`1 - contract.pdf`); `bundle` gives each document a folder of its bundle's files (`1 - contract/`). `certificated`, `certificate-page`, `pades` and `bundle` include only the documents certified so far, and `pades` only those that have one; `404` when no document has the artifact. The artifacts are those of `GET /v1/documents/{documentId}/download/{artifactName}`.

**HTTP parameters:**

```json
[
  {
    "name": "accountId",
    "in": "path",
    "description": "Workspace account ID.",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  {
    "name": "envelopeId",
    "in": "path",
    "description": "The envelope ID.",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  {
    "name": "artifactName",
    "in": "path",
    "description": "Artifact type.",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "original",
        "certificated",
        "certificate-page",
        "pades",
        "bundle"
      ]
    }
  }
]
```

**Request body:** none.

**HTTP 200:** The zip

    "schema": {

  }
}

#### Cancel envelope

**Endpoint:** `PUT /accounts/{accountId}/envelopes/{envelopeId}/cancel`

Cancels every document of the envelope that can still be cancelled, the way deleting a pending document cancels it: the owner, its signers and its copy receivers are told. A document already closed, one every signer has signed (its certification queued), or one being certificated, is left as it is. A notice that fails doesn't stop the other documents. Safe to repeat: a second call cancels nothing and answers each document's status.

**HTTP parameters:**

```json
[
  {
    "name": "accountId",
    "in": "path",
    "description": "Workspace account ID.",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  {
    "name": "envelopeId",
    "in": "path",
    "description": "The envelope ID.",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  }
]
```

**Request body:** none.

**HTTP 200:** What happened to each document, in the envelope's order

```json

  "application/json": {
    "schema": {
      "type": "object",
      "allOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Envelope"
        },
        {
          "properties": {
            "data": {
              "type": "array",
              "items": {
                "required": [
                  "id",
                  "status",
                  "cancelled"
                ],
                "properties": {
                  "id": {
                    "type": "string"
                  },
                  "status": {
                    "description": "The document's status after the call.",
                    "type": "string"
                  },
                  "cancelled": {
                    "description": "Whether this call cancelled it.",
                    "type": "boolean"
                  }
                },
                "type": "object"
              }
            }
          },
          "type": "object"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

#### Remind envelope signer

**Endpoint:** `PUT /accounts/{accountId}/envelopes/{envelopeId}/signers/{signerId}/resend`

Reminds a signer of the whole envelope at once: one message, the envelope's invitation sent again from their first link, listing the documents they still have to sign and, by e-mail, those they already signed. Paid notification methods are charged once for it, for each message that goes out. Each document they still have to sign records a `signature_requested` event, as the invitation does.

]

                  "description": "Whether every notification method sent it, as on a document's reminder.",

#### Add document to envelope

**Endpoint:** `POST /accounts/{accountId}/envelopes/{envelopeId}/documents`

Add a document to an open envelope for some of its signers. The document is checked as on creation: a `metadata_ready` document of the workspace with no assignment, signed on its virtual page or, with `collect`, on the fields placed in `entries`, with its own `expires_at` and `copy_receiver_ids`. An envelope holds up to 20 documents. It charges only its digital-certificate signatures, the envelope having paid for its document and its signers' notifications and identity checks when it was sent, and goes last in the envelope's order; a signer whose identity check the envelope approved is verified on it too. Each chosen signer is sent the envelope's invitation again, through the methods and with the link of their first invitation, now listing the new document, even once the document that invitation was sent from is taken out; the other signers hear nothing. The signers are the envelope's own: no one new joins once it was sent. Needs the Envelopes feature on the workspace's plan, as creating does. A refused request creates and charges nothing; `errors` names every field at fault.

**HTTP parameters:**

```json
[
  {
    "name": "accountId",
    "in": "path",
    "description": "Workspace account ID.",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  {
    "name": "envelopeId",
    "in": "path",
    "description": "The envelope ID.",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  }
]
```

**Full request body contract:**

```json
{
  "required": true,
  "content": {
    "application/json": {
      "schema": {
        "type": "object",
        "allOf": [
          {
            "$ref": "#/components/schemas/EnvelopeDocumentRequest"
          },
          {
            "properties": {
              "max_total_credits": {
                "description": "Optional ceiling on what this request may spend, as on an assignment: refused with `409` and code `CreditCeilingExceeded`, nothing created or charged, when the document costs more on its own. A value that is not a number of credits, zero or more, answers `400`; numeric text counts, as a form-encoded body sends it.",
                "type": "number",
                "minimum": 0
              }
            },
            "type": "object"
          }
        ]
      }
    }
  }
}
```

**HTTP 200:** The envelope with the document added

```json
{
  "application/json": {
    "schema": {
      "type": "object",
      "allOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Envelope"
        },
        {
          "properties": {
            "data": {
              "$ref": "#/components/schemas/DocumentEnvelope"
            }
          },
          "type": "object"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

**HTTP 400:** Refused as sent; nothing was created or charged. Each field at fault is an entry of `errors`: `id` (missing, or a document that can't take the assignment: not found, not `metadata_ready`, or already assigned); `method` (other than `virtual` or `collect`); `entries` (missing for `collect`, or not a list of pages with fields naming their `signer_id`); `signer_ids` (missing, not a list of ids, listed twice, not a signer of the envelope's documents, for `collect` not exactly the signers on its fields, or a digital-certificate signer on a `collect` document or with another signer); `expires_at` (not text, a number or null); a null `field` when the envelope already has 20 documents, or would hold more than 2,000 fields (a virtual document's being one per signer) or more than 100 copy receivers with this one. A page or field the assignment doesn't find on a `collect` document, a balance too low, an `expires_at` or `copy_receiver_ids` the assignment refuses, and a document another request assigned or put back into processing meanwhile answer `400` with `message` only, as on an assignment.

```json
{
  "application/json": {
    "schema": {
      "allOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ErrorEnvelope"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

**HTTP 403:** The workspace's plan lacks the Envelopes feature, or a signer's method or embedded signing the plan lacks (`PlanRestricted`); or an OAuth app's token lacks `documents:write` (`InsufficientScope`). Also a member deactivated in the workspace, a workspace scheduled for deletion, or an OAuth token of another workspace.

```json

      "type": "object",

          "properties": {
            "status": {
              "type": "integer"
            },

              "type": "string",
              "enum": [
                "PlanRestricted",
                "InsufficientScope"
              ]
            }
          },
          "type": "object"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

#### Take document out of envelope

**Endpoint:** `DELETE /accounts/{accountId}/envelopes/{envelopeId}/documents/{documentId}`

Take an open document out of the envelope: it is cancelled as cancelling the envelope cancels it, its owner, signers and copy receivers told in a notice naming the envelope, and it is no longer listed; the other documents carry on. Out of the envelope, `GET /v1/documents/{documentId}?expand=envelope_id` reads null for it; a signer whose invitation link was sent from it keeps reaching the envelope's other documents with that link, so it can't be deleted on its own (`409` with code `EnvelopeOpen`) until every document of the envelope is closed. A document that is not in the envelope, or was taken out already, answers `404`.

**HTTP parameters:**

```json
[
  {
    "name": "accountId",
    "in": "path",
    "description": "Workspace account ID.",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  {
    "name": "envelopeId",
    "in": "path",
    "description": "The envelope ID.",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  {
    "name": "documentId",
    "in": "path",
    "description": "Document ID.",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  }
]
```

**Request body:** none.

**HTTP 200:** The envelope without the document

}

### Embedded Signing

List/Add/Update/Remove Origin use the credential's Account ID and require an API key. Add/Update take **Origin**; Update/Remove require **Origin ID**. Create Session takes Document ID, Assignment ID and Signer ID and accepts API key or OAuth `documents:write`. It requires a signer created with `embedded: true` and an allowed origin. Only Bypass, Email and Whatsapp support embedded signing; biometric and digital-certificate signers use the hosted page. List returns one item per origin; mutations return the unwrapped response.

Create Session returns a secret, single-use iframe URL expiring in five minutes. Keep it out of logs, load it only on an allowed origin and mint another for a new opening. The node never retries this creation automatically. `/embed/sessions/{token}/exchange` belongs to the hosted Assinafy iframe page and is intentionally not an integration operation. Do not call it to extract signer credentials.

#### List embed origins

**Endpoint:** `GET /accounts/{accountId}/embed-origins`

List the origins allowed to embed the workspace's signing pages, oldest first. Returns `403` with code `PlanRestricted` when the workspace's plan does not include embedded signing. Not available to OAuth applications.

#### Add embed origin

**Endpoint:** `POST /accounts/{accountId}/embed-origins`

Allow one more origin to embed the workspace's signing pages. An origin is an exact `scheme://host[:port]`: `https` (or `http` for `localhost` and `127.0.0.1` only), with no path, query, fragment, credentials or wildcard. The host is lowercased and a trailing `/` and the default port are dropped. Returns `400` when the origin is invalid, already registered, or the workspace already has 10 origins. Returns `403` with code `PlanRestricted` when the workspace's plan does not include embedded signing. Not available to OAuth applications.

#### Create an embed session

**Endpoint:** `POST /documents/{documentId}/assignments/{assignmentId}/signers/{signerId}/embed-sessions`

Mint a single-use embed URL for a signer created with `embedded: true`. Load the URL in an iframe on one of the workspace's embed origins within 5 minutes; opening it spends the token, so mint a fresh one each time the signer opens the page. The signer's access code is never returned. Refused with `400` (`embed_not_enabled_for_signer`) for a signer that is not embedded, `409` when the signer already finished (`already_signed`), the document is still being uploaded or processed (`document_not_ready`: retry in a moment), the document can no longer be signed or the signer's step has not started (`document_unavailable`), or the workspace has no embed origin (`embed_origins_missing`), `403` with code `PlanRestricted` when the plan does not include embedded signing, and `429` after 30 sessions for one signer in an hour.

**HTTP parameters:**

```json
[
  {

### Workspace notification controls and usage

**Workspace → Get Usage**, **Get Notification Policy**, **Update Notification Policy**, **Get Push Preferences** and **Update Push Preferences** require API-key authentication and reject OAuth before any request. Usage/policy use **Workspace ID** or the credential's Account ID; push preferences apply to the authenticated user. Updates take **Notification Settings (JSON)** (`notificationSettings`), send only the supplied fields and preserve false/null/nested values. Owner-only policy changes remain enforced by the API. Each operation emits one item with the unwrapped payload.

#### Get account usage

**Endpoint:** `GET /accounts/{accountId}/usage`

The workspace's plan and what is left of it, for a plan and credit meter. Nothing about prices or payments: those stay in **Get current subscription**. Not available to third-party OAuth applications (`403`), whatever their scopes: this is billing data. Assinafy's own apps can read it with the `account:read` scope.

**HTTP parameters:**

```json
[
  {
    "name": "accountId",
    "in": "path",
    "description": "Workspace account ID.",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string"

#### Update the workspace notification policy

**Endpoint:** `PUT /accounts/{accountId}/notification-policy`

Owners only (`403` for other members). Merges the supplied fields: send only `events` keys you want to change, and/or `owners_receive_all`. An event set to `false` reaches nobody in this workspace, on any channel, whatever each member's own preferences; the identity review e-mail is the exception and is always sent, because the signer cannot continue without it. An unknown field or code, a non-boolean value or an empty body is rejected with `400` and nothing is written. Returns the full policy.

**HTTP parameters:**

```json

  {
    "name": "accountId",
    "in": "path",

    "schema": {

]

### Signer branding and certified copies

**Signer Document → Get Signing Theme**, **Download Signing Logo** and **Send Certified Copy** require **Signer Access Code** and send no workspace authentication. Send Certified Copy optionally accepts **Document ID** (`certifiedCopyDocumentId`) to select another document in the same envelope. **Download Certified Copy** and **Resend Certified Copy** require **Download Token** instead, with no signer code or workspace authentication. Download Certified Copy supports **PAdES**; binary operations use **Put Output In Field** (default `data`). The token never appears in binary metadata. Expired-link redirects are not followed, and HTML responses are rejected instead of being saved as PDFs/images.

Copy requests send to the original signing contact, not a later changed signer record. Completion links last seven days, resent links 30 minutes. Email resend is limited to once per two minutes and ten times per 24 hours; WhatsApp permits one resend per signature request. Sending requires completed signing and certification; check `certified_copy` from Get Sign Page. Protect all codes and download links as bearer credentials.

#### Workspace brand for the signing page

**Endpoint:** `GET /sign/theme`

The brand of the workspace that sent the document: its name, its colors (`primary_color` is the button background, `secondary_color` the button text, 6-digit hex without `#`) and the address of its logo, or `null` when it has none. The logo address carries no access code: append `signer-access-code` to it, as to every signer call. Available before verification and after the document is deleted, so every screen of the signing page can carry the brand. Uses the signer access code.

**Request body:** none.

**HTTP 200:** The brand

```json
{
  "application/json": {
    "schema": {
      "type": "object",
      "allOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Envelope"
        },
        {
          "properties": {
            "data": {
              "$ref": "#/components/schemas/AccountTheme"
            }
          },
          "type": "object"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

**HTTP 400:** With a signer access code: the signer's step has not started yet (sequential signing).

      "type": "object",

  }
}

#### Send the certified copy again

**Endpoint:** `POST /sign/certified-copy`

Sends the signer their certified document again, with a download link valid for 30 minutes. It goes on the channel of the signature request (e-mail when the request used e-mail, otherwise WhatsApp) and only to the e-mail or number the signing messages went to, never to a changed signer record. Works with the signer's access code after they signed, including once the code is closed and after the workspace is deleted. E-mail: once every 2 minutes and 10 times in 24 hours. WhatsApp: once per signature request; after that the signer must ask the sender. `certified_copy` on `GET /v1/sign` says whether it is available.

**Full request body contract:**

```json
{
  "required": false,
  "content": {
    "application/json": {
      "schema": {
        "properties": {
          "document_id": {
            "description": "With the code of a document of an envelope: another document of that envelope the signer is on, whose certified copy to send, in place of the code's own. A value that is no document id (a list, or empty) answers `400`. Ignored with any other code.",
            "type": "string"
          }
        },
        "type": "object"
      }
    }
  }
}
```

**HTTP 200:** Sent

```json
{
  "application/json": {
    "schema": {
      "type": "object",
      "allOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Envelope"
        },
        {
          "properties": {
            "data": {
              "properties": {
                "available": {
                  "description": "Whether it can be asked for again.",
                  "type": "boolean"
                },
                "notification_method": {
                  "type": "string",
                  "enum": [
                    "Email",
                    "Whatsapp"
                  ]
                },
                "resends_left": {
                  "description": "WhatsApp re-sends left; null for e-mail.",
                  "type": [
                    "integer",
                    "null"
                  ]
                },
                "sent_to": {
                  "description": "The masked contact it went to.",
                  "type": "string"
                }
              },
              "type": "object"
            }
          },
          "type": "object"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

**HTTP 404:** `document_id` names a document outside the code's envelope, or one the signer is not on.

```json
{
  "application/json": {
    "schema": {
      "type": "object",
      "allOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ErrorEnvelope"
        },
        {
          "properties": {
            "status": {
              "type": "integer"
            },
            "message": {
              "type": "string"
            }
          },
          "type": "object"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

**HTTP 409:** The signer has not signed, the document is not certified yet, or the request has no e-mail or WhatsApp to send to (ask the sender).

```json
{
  "application/json": {
    "schema": {
      "type": "object",
      "allOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ErrorEnvelope"
        },
        {
          "properties": {
            "status": {
              "type": "integer"
            },

              "type": "string"
            }
          },
          "type": "object"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

#### Download the certified copy from a sent link

**Endpoint:** `GET /signers/downloads/{token}`

The link in the completion and re-send messages. Valid 7 days in the completion message and 30 minutes when re-sent. Add `/pades` for the PAdES version on documents that have one. An expired or unknown link is redirected (`302`) to the expired-link page; for a known link the redirect carries `?token=`, with which the page can ask for a new one (`POST /v1/signers/downloads/{token}/resend`).

**HTTP parameters:**

```json
[
  {
    "name": "token",
    "in": "path",
    "description": "The link token.",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  }
]
```

**Request body:** none.

**HTTP 200:** The certified PDF

```json
{
  "application/pdf": {
    "schema": {
      "type": "string",
      "format": "binary"
    }
  }
}
```

**HTTP 302:** Expired or unknown link: redirected to the expired-link page.

**HTTP 404:** `/pades` on a document that has no PAdES version.

```json
{
  "application/json": {
    "schema": {
      "type": "object",
      "allOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ErrorEnvelope"
        },
        {
          "properties": {
            "status": {
              "type": "integer"
            },
            "message": {
              "type": "string"
            }
          },
          "type": "object"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

**HTTP 500:** Unexpected server error.

```json
{
  "application/json": {
    "schema": {
      "type": "object",
      "allOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/ErrorEnvelope"
        },

            "status": {
              "type": "integer"
            },

              "type": "string"
            }
          },
          "type": "object"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

#### Send the certified copy again from an expired link

**Endpoint:** `POST /signers/downloads/{token}/resend`

Used by the page an expired download link leads to. Works with the link's token even after it expired, and on the same terms as `POST /v1/sign/certified-copy`: the request's channel, only the e-mail or number of the signing, the same limits. Copy receivers' links have no signature request (`409`).

**HTTP parameters:**

```json
[
  {
    "name": "token",
    "in": "path",
    "description": "The link token.",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  }
]
```

**Request body:** none.

**HTTP 200:** Sent

```json
{
  "application/json": {
    "schema": {
      "type": "object",
      "allOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Envelope"
        },
        {
          "properties": {
            "data": {
              "properties": {
                "available": {
                  "type": "boolean"
                },
                "notification_method": {
                  "type": "string",
                  "enum": [
                    "Email",
                    "Whatsapp"
                  ]
                },
                "resends_left": {
                  "type": [
                    "integer",
                    "null"
                  ]
                },
                "sent_to": {
                  "type": "string"
                }
              },
              "type": "object"
            }
          },
          "type": "object"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

**HTTP 404:** Unknown link.

**HTTP 409:** Nothing can be sent from here (copy receiver, Bypass signer, no recorded contact, or the document is not certified): ask the sender.

**HTTP 429:** E-mail: asked again within 2 minutes or more than 10 times in 24 hours. WhatsApp: already re-sent once.

**HTTP 500:** Unexpected server error.

```json

            },

            }
          },
          "type": "object"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}

### Webhook test and secret rotation

**Webhook → Test Endpoint** requires Endpoint ID and API key or OAuth `webhooks:write`; it sends one `webhook_test` delivery and does not subscribe the endpoint to that event. **Rotate Endpoint Signing Secret** accepts **Keep Previous Secret** (`keepPreviousSecret`, default false), sent as `keep_previous`. True keeps the old key valid for 24 hours; persist the new secret and `previous_secret_expires_at` securely. The Trigger accepts any matching `v1` signature, with its documented process cache of up to 60 seconds.

#### Send a test delivery

**Endpoint:** `POST /accounts/{accountId}/webhooks/endpoints/{endpointId}/test`

Sends the endpoint one `webhook_test` event now, signed and sent like any delivery, and returns the entry of that attempt (`kind: manual`, also listed in the delivery history). It goes to this endpoint only, whatever events it subscribes to, and also while the endpoint is paused or inactive; it neither lifts a pause nor counts toward one. The endpoint has 10 seconds to answer. Its `payload` and `origin` are `null`; the endpoint object carries its id, name, url, events and status, never its secret. No endpoint can subscribe to `webhook_test`, and it is not listed in `GET /webhooks/event-types`. Its `payload` and `origin` are `null`; the endpoint object carries its id, name, url, events and status, never its secret. No endpoint can subscribe to `webhook_test`, and it is not listed in `GET /webhooks/event-types`. The event names the endpoint as its `subject` and `object` and carries nothing about the workspace beyond `account_id`: no billing and no members. Up to 10 tests per user every 10 minutes. Requires the `webhooks:write` OAuth scope.

**HTTP parameters:**

```json
[
  {
    "name": "accountId",
    "in": "path",
    "description": "Workspace account ID.",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  },
  {
    "name": "endpointId",
    "in": "path",
    "description": "The webhook endpoint ID.",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "type": "string"
    }
  }
]
```

**Request body:** none.

**HTTP 200:** The entry of the test delivery

```json
{
  "application/json": {
    "schema": {
      "type": "object",
      "allOf": [
        {
          "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Envelope"
        },
        {
          "properties": {
            "data": {
              "$ref": "#/components/schemas/WebhookDispatch"
            }
          },
          "type": "object"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

**HTTP 404:** The requested resource does not exist.

            },

### Optional fields on existing operations

- **Document → Get**: Expand can request `envelope_id` and/or `envelope`; the query joins values with commas. Omission preserves the ordinary response.
- **Assignment → Create** and **Document → Create From Template**: each signer accepts `embedded` (false is preserved). Additional Fields → Maximum Total Credits sends `max_total_credits`, including zero; omission leaves the API budget unspecified. Template Create also exposes Idempotency Key. Estimate operations do not send these creation-only fields.
- **Assignment → Get Sign Page**: Sign Page Options → Document ID sends `document_id` to select another document in the code's envelope; Expand Envelope adds `expand=envelope`. **Signer Document → Get Current** also supports Expand Envelope. A signer response contains only that signer's permitted state, never another signer's access code.
- **Signer Document → List/Search**: Envelope ID sends `envelope_id` alongside the existing filters and explicit authentication selection.
- **Workspace → Get**: Expand Signing Features sends `expand=signing_features`; the response reports method/feature entitlements.
- **Contract → Get Related**: Expand Document sends `expand=document` to include the related document details.


#### Rotate Endpoint Signing Secret payloads

`POST /accounts/{accountId}/webhooks/endpoints/{endpointId}/secret/rotate`

Replace the endpoint's signing secret and return the new one. The old secret stops working immediately, so update your receiver right away; or send `keep_previous: true` to keep it signing deliveries beside the new one for 24 hours (`previous_secret_expires_at`): during that time `webhook-signature` carries both signatures, so your receiver can switch to the new secret without failing verification. Only the secret just replaced is kept: rotating again within those 24 hours stops the one kept before. Two rotations sent at once take effect one after the other, each answering the secret it set. Returns `400` when signing is disabled or `keep_previous` is not a boolean. Requires the `webhooks:write` OAuth scope.

**Request:**

```json
{
  "required": false,
  "content": {
    "application/json": {
      "schema": {
        "properties": {
          "keep_previous": {
            "description": "`true`: the replaced secret keeps signing for 24 hours. Default `false`: it stops at once.",
            "type": "boolean"
          }
        },
        "type": "object"
      }
    }
  }
}
```

**Success response:**

```json
{
  "description": "The new secret",
  "content": {
    "application/json": {
      "schema": {
        "type": "object",
        "allOf": [
          {
            "$ref": "#/components/schemas/Envelope"
          },
          {
            "properties": {
              "data": {
                "type": "object",
                "allOf": [
                  {
                    "$ref": "#/components/schemas/WebhookEndpointSecret"
                  },
                  {
                    "properties": {
                      "previous_secret_expires_at": {
                        "description": "Only with `keep_previous: true`: until when the secret replaced keeps signing deliveries beside this one.",
                        "type": "string",
                        "format": "date-time"
                      }
                    },
                    "type": "object"
                  }
                ]
              }
            },
            "type": "object"
          }
        ]
      }
    }
  }
}
```
